(b) ⭐ **A FLOOR / CLAMP / DEFAULT ON A DISPLAYED NUMBER MUST REPORT WHETHER IT BOUND** — `max(metered, $20)` made 'measured $20' and 'invented $20' pixel-identical, so neither the operator nor the page could tell; a served field needs a companion `*_floored` flag. Generalizes to EVERY fallback/default/clamp on a served number — a fallback that can't announce itself is indistinguishable from a measurement (sibling of the 'a fallback message must not double as a negative result' / `|| echo 0` class). (c) A THIRD-PARTY TOKEN'S SCOPE CAN DENY A WHOLE ENDPOINT CLASS, and it fails into your GRACEFUL-DEGRADE path = a mis-scoped credential looks EXACTLY like an unconfigured one (Vercel billing is TEAM-level; a project-scoped token silently 403s the whole billing endpoint) → provisioning docs must name the required SCOPE, not just the variable. ⚠️ deploy-ops sibling: a repo `.env` does NOTHING for the API Lambda — `deploy.sh` never touches the function's env config; a new token must be set ON THE FUNCTION (console or `update-function-configuration`, which REPLACES the whole Variables map).
- **🧊 A TRANSITIVE MODULE-SCOPE IMPORT SILENTLY REINTRODUCES A HEAVY DEP ON THE LAMBDA IMPORT PATH — and Lambda MEMORY does NOT fix an I/O-BOUND cold-start init (PERF, 2026-08-11):** the API Lambda's cold init was ~4s because `main.py` imports every router to register it → the admin/finances/pipeline routers imported `services/snowflake.py` at MODULE scope → `snowflake.connector` → pandas → pyarrow, so EVERY caller paid for 3 admin endpoints. ⭐ Snowflake was never on a REQUEST path (the repo rule held) — it was on the IMPORT path, an invariant the codebase BELIEVED it had (`fantasy_import_telemetry.py` documents 'no pandas/pyarrow in this Lambda') and had silently lost through a transitive import. Cure = lazy the heavy imports; −21.8% on every cold start. RULES: (a) guard the import path with a test that imports the app IN A SUBPROCESS (in-process is VACUOUS — pytest itself loads pandas, so it answers about the test runner) + asserts non-vacuity first (a crashed import also has no pandas), and use an AST check not grep (the module's docstring NAMES `snowflake.connector` while telling you not to import it — grep false-matches the prose); `test_api_cold_start_imports.py` is the reference. (b) ⭐ MORE LAMBDA MEMORY DOES NOT SPEED AN I/O-BOUND INIT — a 512→1536MB bump projected to cut init ~3× (assumed CPU-bound imports) measured +0.2% and was reverted; init was I/O-bound on UNPACKING a 57 MB package, not CPU. Don't assume a memory bump fixes cold start — measure; the lever for an unpack-bound init is package pruning or provisioned concurrency, not vCPU. SIBLING (correctness): a coverage/verdict panel that treats a MISSING/errored fetch as 'everything applies' silently makes a FALSE claim on any upstream outage — it must WAIT for real columns and name its non-answers (checking / could-not-check), never render an optimistic default (the honest-empty-state class).
- **🔑 COGNITO GOTCHAS THAT MAKE A SECURITY GATE READ-ENABLED-BUT-ENFORCE-NOTHING, AND A GROUP THE ONLY USABLE SIGNAL (G100-C0-MFA, 2026-08-13):** (a) ⭐ **`cognito:groups` arrives BRACKETED + SPACE-SEPARATED even for ONE group** — `[subscriber]`, `[subscriber passwordless]`, or `null` for no groups — NOT comma-separated. A guard that split on `,` matched NOTHING, so with enforcement ON it read as enabled and gated NOBODY. ⚠️ the pre-existing tests PASSED because they were written with the comma form = restating the parser's own assumption (the INC-38/NF-C0e vacuous-test class) → test the claim against a REAL token shape, never the shape the code assumes. (b) ⭐ **this pool's ACCESS token carries NO `amr` and NO `identities` on ANY door** (password, Google, fresh CUSTOM_AUTH all return `amr: []`) — the frontend sends the access token, so an `amr`-based exemption is DEAD CODE in prod and a Cognito GROUP was the only server-verifiable signal. ⇒ before building an auth check on a token claim, verify what the token ACTUALLY carries (a self-only `/auth/session-diagnostics` endpoint reporting post-authorizer claims + the guard's verdict made both acceptance legs answerable without a real subscriber). (c) **a `guard_version` claim marker beats 'the PR merged' for an API-Lambda change** — no CD there, so a merged PR is NOT a deployed build; the marker caught the first verification 404ing on a deploy of a `dev` that didn't yet contain the PR. (d) the E9.8 flip's frontend half is the documented Vercel trap — `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENFORCE_SUBSCRIBER_MFA=1` is an env var, changing it doesn't change git, so a plain Redeploy skips the build via `ignoreCommand` and the value never takes effect: untick 'Use project's Ignore Build Step' (docs/vercel_build_skipping.md).
- **🧮 THERE IS NO 'FREE OPTIMIZER RUN' — THE DRAFT OPTIMIZER IS FULLY PAID; the only free fantasy quota is ONE SAVED LEAGUE (Draft-optimizer check, 2026-08-12):** multiple cards/docs asserted 'free = 1 optimizer run' — FALSE: no run-counting exists anywhere in the codebase, the optimizer is server-gated on `require_fantasy_access` (0 free runs), and the ONLY free quota is `FREE_PERSONALIZED_LEAGUE_QUOTA=1` (one saved personalized league). ⛔ don't build a run-limit UI or gate against a free optimizer run. SIBLINGS from the same story: (a) ⭐ **A CONTRACT CHANGE THAT GIVES A PLACEHOLDER REAL VALUES BREAKS EVERY CONSUMER STILL ASSUMING THE PLACEHOLDER** — NF1.6 gave K/DST real projections, but 3 separate places still assumed the pre-NF1.6 null-VOR world (a scarcity bonus keyed on the tier cliff not the best player at the position → 445 intra-position inversions; a draft that could END with empty mandatory starters; K/DST recommended in early rounds). When a field goes null-placeholder → real, grep every consumer that branched on 'is it null'. (b) **TWO SURFACES THAT ORDER THE SAME LIST MUST SHARE ONE ORDERING FUNCTION** — the recommender fix left the best-available board sorting itself INLINE in the component, so a user declining a recommendation was one click from the exact advice we'd just decided to withhold; the fix moved ordering into ONE exported `sortAvailable` both surfaces call (the E9.61 'two renderers of one field are two rule sets' lesson, on the ordering side). (c) the ranker/optimizer keeps LOCK-STEP: `frontend/lib/draft-optimizer.ts` + `fantasy_engine/draft.py` change together on every fix. All 4 defects were found by driving the REAL shipping engine against LIVE payloads, not fixtures.
- **🖨️ EIGHT NF-C4 DEFECTS, ALL GREEN IN CI, ALL VISIBLE ONLY IN RENDERED OUTPUT — the suite was asserting on SOURCE (NF-C4, 2026-08-18):** a frontend guard that greps source (or walks className strings) tests that someone TYPED a string, not that the page RENDERS correctly. Reusable clusters: **(a) PRINTING A DARK WEB APP** — browsers do NOT print background colours, so a dark site prints unreadably (cure = ONE `globals.css` colour-reset rule under `@media print`, never a `print:text-black` per span the next component misses); a root-layout `SiteFooter` is a SIBLING of every page → no page-level `print:hidden` can reach it (needs a stylesheet rule keyed on a marker the page renders); a white-on-dark EXTERNAL-SVG logo in an `<img>` prints as NOTHING and CSS can't recolour it (ship a `black-logo` variant, eager-loaded because its container is `display:none` until print); ASSERT all of this via `page.emulateMedia({media:'print'})` computed-layout checks (nav-hidden, footer-hidden, `rgb(0,0,0)` text, black logo), NOT by grepping the print block for 'footer'+'display:none' (a broken selector satisfies that). **(b) A CONTROLLED-INPUT PER-KEYSTROKE TRANSFORM** — Playwright's `fill()` sets the whole value in ONE event, structurally blind to a per-keystroke bug (the note box trimmed each keystroke → every space eaten); and a pure test replaying `typed.slice(0,i)` ends on the FULL string (nothing to trim) — a controlled input reads its value back out of STATE, so the test must model read-back-from-state AND drive keystroke-by-keystroke. **(c) `{EXPR} word` IN JSX TRIMS THE LEADING SPACE** ('our 2026board') — recurs anywhere in `frontend/` that writes `{EXPR} word`; fix with a single template literal. ⭐ THREE guards were VACUOUS on first cut, found ONLY by the red proof (one satisfied by a type-union member that no longer rendered; one by an identifier surviving in a dependency array; one by 'footer'+'display:none' both appearing under a broken selector) → a frontend guard must assert COMPUTED LAYOUT / rendered output and be RED-proven, or it tests the author's typing. ⭐ PROCESS reconfirmations: a STANDING PR can merge an EARLIER snapshot (#922 merged at a PR `head.sha` 3 commits behind the pushed tip → dev briefly shipped the broken note box while the PR read merged+green; check `gh api .../pulls/N -q .head.sha` vs `git rev-parse HEAD` before trusting green — the E9.60 stale-head family); and a new REQUEST field silently deploy-skews (older backend accepts+ignores → 200 → '✓Saved' then gone on reload, E8.6) — the save now compares returned-vs-sent and the E2E drives the skew as a first-class mock mode.
- **💵 AN AUCTION DOLLAR IS A SHARE OF A FIXED POOL — a per-player QUANTILE band CANNOT be converted to it, and a REPLACEMENT LEVEL at the STARTER cutoff over-concentrates value (NF-C5, 2026-08-18):** the auction optimizer published an 80% dollar BAND (aucLo/aucHi); measured on board_half_ppr_12 (870 rows, $2,400 pool) the low edges summed to $180 (7.5% of the pool — all $1) and the high edges to $9,882 (412%), because each quantile edge prices a season quantile through a RATE + a REPLACEMENT LEVEL belonging to a DIFFERENT world, while a dollar must be a share of ONE fixed pool. Both obvious repairs fail: narrower p25/p75 only shrinks the symptom; a common-quantile world (everyone at p10, replacement recomputed there) restores CONSERVATION but is NOT ORDERED — replacement collapses 124.6→5.5 and top players price BELOW their point value. RULE: a dollar BAND needs a model of how players' seasons CO-MOVE; without one, publish ONE value and keep the interval in its NATIVE unit (points), never converted to a share-of-fixed-pool currency (conservation and ordering can't both hold under a naive per-player quantile→$). ⭐ SIBLING (replacement level) — the auction point VALUE over-concentrated (top player 56.5% of a $200 budget vs a 20–50% face-validity band) because replacement sat at the STARTER cutoff (~107 above-replacement players vs 180 roster spots → ~76 draftable players price at exactly $1, so the whole surplus piles into the top ~107). For an AUCTION the replacement level is the ROSTER-FILL cutoff (you buy ~180 players), NOT the snake-VOR starter cutoff — a starter-cutoff replacement carried into an auction is the bug, and fixing it (not tuning toward what a room pays — the live inflation multiplier expresses that gap) is the honest repair. ⭐ SIBLING (E2.1-r + armchair) — the face-validity test passed only because its synthetic board differs from production; the production breach is RECORDED IN THE TEST DOCSTRING, not accommodated (re-deriving a plausibility bar from the number that failed it is the E2.1-r inversion). And auction.py's docstring had reasoned the OPPOSITE from the armchair ('a common-quantile world would barely move the values') — the measurement refuted it: measure a valuation, never reason it from the armchair (the ESPN-pruner / re-measure family).
- **🔒 A RESPONSE-BODY READ CAN CARRY A CREDENTIAL/PII EVEN WHEN A SIBLING ENDPOINT DOESN'T — 'the body can't hold the cookie' is an ENDPOINT fact, not a general one; a passive-observe reader needs a FAIL-CLOSED HOST ALLOWLIST (NF-C-LDA-0, 2026-08-18):** the Live-Draft-Assistant spike proved NF-C0's premise ('espn_s2 is never echoed into a response body → a body paste can't carry the credential') FALSE for the draft room as a whole — the room calls a Disney identity service whose BODY returns the session token alongside the user's name/email/DOB/account-id. One capture held the operator's PII; the credential escaped capture ONLY BY LUCK (our summarizer truncates long strings and the token exceeded the threshold — a shorter one would have been verbatim). RULES: (a) a summarizer/truncation is NOT a privacy control; (b) a passive reader (extension/proxy/capture tool) must FAIL-CLOSED on a HOST/ENDPOINT ALLOWLIST — read bodies only from the allowlisted hosts, and for everything else record THAT a call happened, never WHAT came back (three separate capture rounds each carried an unexpected secret — incl. a line-protocol handshake token under every length threshold a JSON-shaped rule could see — so the allowlist must be STRUCTURAL, not a growing patch list); (c) the safety of a reader that relies on 'this endpoint's body has no credential' is LOAD-BEARING on WHICH ENDPOINT — document it (the existing ESPN import is safe because it reads ONE league URL that genuinely carries no credential, not from any general property); (d) OBSERVE, NEVER ORIGINATE — a content script CAN make an authenticated request with the user's cookie attached automatically, a DIFFERENT compliance line than 'never hold the cookie' (NF-C0); make originating a BUILD FAILURE. ⚠️ our agent is also robots-blocked at ESPN (`User-agent: anthropic-ai / Disallow: /`) — a hard stop, so the session cannot fetch the page itself; all data came from operator-produced captures. ⭐ SIBLING (a fetched-once payload's 'live' array can be a permanently PRE-EVENT snapshot): ESPN's league JSON `picks[]` NEVER populates — fetched once at load, never re-polled → polling it for picks returns a confidently-wrong pre-draft snapshot with no error; the live state is ENTIRELY on the WebSocket → SEED from the snapshot, APPLY socket deltas (proven by a positive control that fired on a DIFFERENT changing endpoint, not this one). ⭐ SIBLING (entity resolution): an id-crosswalk that misses the ROOKIE class (nflverse assigns no ESPN id until an NFL-roster appearance) fails SILENTLY on exactly the players a draft cares about while reporting 90% — a resolver needs a stable-id → name+pos+team ladder, and TWO independent paths agreeing (0 disagreements over 4,549 rows) is what makes the number trustworthy, not the point estimate.
- **📊 A VENDOR API LABELLING FIELDS 'restricted' DESCRIBES THE RESPONSE, NOT YOUR ENTITLEMENT — and a short-lived JWT behind a REFRESH cookie has NO bearer token to copy (NF-W9-0, 2026-08-18):** PFF's /facet/* JSON returns a reduced field set + marks the rest 'restricted'; that read FIRST as a subscription paywall and was WRONG — the account's OWN UI export contained all 28 'restricted' fields → the full set was ONE query param away (`&export=true` → a CSV with 47 columns incl. routes/aDOT/yprr). RULE: don't infer an entitlement limit from a reduced API payload — validate against what the account's own UI can export; the reduced JSON is a RESPONSE-SHAPE default, not a paywall. ⭐ AUTH PATTERN (Clerk / refresh-token APIs): there is NO bearer token to copy — the `__session` cookie is a JWT with a ~60-SECOND lifetime (a cookie pasted from DevTools is already expired, which reads as 'I can't find the auth token'), minted on demand from a long-lived `__refresh_*` cookie via a 307 handshake. A stateless `Cookie:` header LOOPS on the handshake → use a cookie-PERSISTING, redirect-following session; an hours-old operator cookie works because the refresh cookie does the real work (capture once by hand). ⭐ SIBLING (entity resolution): a vendor id populated PER-ROSTER-ROW (PFF `pff_id`) makes a same-season join drop 44% while a cross-season player-level collapse reaches ≥99.6% — but `max(pff_id)` is a WRONG MERGE (one id mapped two players) → DROP ambiguous ids, never arbitrate (⛔ and never blanket-strip 'State' from a school name — Ohio/Ohio State coexist). ⚠️ THE ACTUAL BLOCKER IS LICENSING, NOT ENGINEERING: bulk-extracting a competitor's PAID data into a PAID product (past explicit DataDome bot protection, which signals intent) is a business/legal decision to settle BEFORE any ingest — the legitimate path may be a CONVERSATION with the vendor's licensing arm, not a scraper.
- **🎲 A DETERMINISTIC-REPLAY SIM MUST DERIVE EACH STEP'S RNG FROM `(seed, step_index, actor)`, NEVER A GENERATOR CARRIED ACROSS THE BATCH — a shared generator is the OBVIOUS way to write it and fails with NO SYMPTOM (NF-C2.1, 2026-08-17):** the mock-draft simulator's 'Skip to my pick' fast-forward, mid-draft reload survival (localStorage), and Undo ALL work for one reason — every CPU pick seeds a FRESH RNG on `(seed, overallPick, slot)` and nothing carries RNG state across picks; a single generator threaded through the pick loop would produce a DIFFERENT draft on replay/skip/reload with no error, no crash, just a silently non-reproducible room. ⭐ ARCHITECTURAL CONSTRAINT for any successor (the 'Draft Lab' rewrite): every stateful concept a richer CPU wants — room climate, run pressure, an evolving strategy posterior — MUST be a PURE FUNCTION of `(seed, picks-so-far)`, not accumulated mutable state, or the replay guarantee breaks; designing it in is cheap, retrofitting after building stateful CPUs is a full rewrite. ⭐ SIBLING (honest-analytics) — **a grade that ranks a field on the SAME projections your recommender MAXIMIZES is CIRCULAR by construction — label it so, UNCONDITIONALLY:** the room-rank grade scores every team on our projected starter points while the CPUs draft partly off market ADP, so a user who follows our recommendations finishes top BY CONSTRUCTION → 'you finished 1st of 12' is a near-worthless signal; the screen carries a verbatim `GRADE_CIRCULARITY_NOTE` (test-asserted on the shipped surface). It gets WORSE, not better, as CPUs move to a utility function sharing terms with `recommend` (more correlation with our own advice) → imperfect-beliefs work is an explicit V1 success criterion, not a footnote (the `best_alpha=0` / no-win-claims ethos, on the fantasy side). ⭐ SIBLING (layout) — **a CSS grid `1fr` track's automatic minimum is its MIN-CONTENT width, and Tailwind `truncate` sets `white-space:nowrap`, so a long unbroken sentence GROWS the track and gives the WHOLE PAGE a horizontal scrollbar** (measured scrollWidth 2129 at a 1512px viewport, roster panel shoved off-screen); cure = `min-w-0` on the grid item — needed on BOTH the sim and the live optimizer (same bug, two surfaces). ⭐ SIBLING (metric sign) — **a signed 'distance from market' shipped INVERTED presents the biggest REACH as the biggest VALUE:** `marketRank − overallPick` (positive for a reach) filed a 50-pick reach (Jordyn Tyson #44 vs ADP 94) under 'Fell furthest past ADP'; use `overallPick − adp`, and render the player's OWN adp, never a dense 1..226 rank under an 'ADP' label (the E9.61 two-renderers / label-mismatch family).
- **🔐 SIX THINGS EVERY FUTURE FANTASY FRONTEND STORY MUST KNOW AFTER THE ENTITLEMENT SPLIT (NF-EPIC 1, 2026-08-11):** (a) ⭐ **A GATED ROUTE PLACED UNDER A PUBLIC PATH PREFIX SILENTLY INHERITS THAT PREFIX'S CDN CACHE RULE + DEGRADE ENTRY** — `/fantasy/nfl/projections/full` inherited `/fantasy/nfl/projections`'s public cache; caught only because a test asserted it directly. A gated route must NOT be a child of a public path (the fix used a sibling `/projections-full`); ⛔ never add a gated route to the CDN allowlist / public cache / degrade floor. (b) ⭐ **FRONTEND-FIRST for a change that REMOVES data the deployed client depends on** — NF-EPIC 1 shipped backend-first and left a window where My League rendered a silently-ZEROED board (the old client scored from a stat line that was gone); frontend-first yields an honest 'couldn't score' error instead of wrong numbers. (c) **THE PAID SUBSTRATE MOVED**: per-stat/scorable data (`fpStd`/`fpHalf`/raw stat line) is now on the entitled `/fantasy/nfl/projections-full`; the public `/fantasy/nfl/projections` carries identity + PPR number + 80% range + ADP + `contrib` ONLY. Client-side league scoring is GONE and cannot return — any re-score for a user's settings is SERVER-SIDE (`/fantasy/nfl/league-board`, ONE league at a time; `/my-teams` returns roster rows only — 25 leagues × 858 exceeds Lambda's 6 MB response cap). (d) **`STAT_FIELD` IS THE PAYWALL** — the paid set is derived from the scorer's own map, so ADDING A SCORABLE STAT AUTOMATICALLY MAKES IT PAID (deliberate — a hand list would leak the next field silently); making a new stat public is a PRICING decision + explicit exception, not a config tweak. (e) **THREE implementations of one scoring policy now exist** (`fantasy_engine` = authority, the browser TS port, the Lambda Python scorer); ⭐ any scoring-rule change must move ALL THREE, and `test_nf_epic1_parity.py` is a MERGE GATE demanding exact agreement — budget this tax into any scoring-touching story (it already caught 32 wrong ranks). (f) ⛔ **do NOT claim 'zero leak'** — a free account can still probe stats one saved league at a time (~50 rate-limited authenticated round trips, `sub`-attributable) = bounded, NOT closed (`docs/nf_epic1_entitlement_audit.md` §9). Sibling gotchas: a stale `next-server` squatting port 3100 silently hijacks the whole E2E suite (check the port first); and `MEANINGFUL_MOVE` (the 1.7 vs-generic delta threshold) hasn't been re-measured since the engine swap — check it first if the delta column behaves oddly.
- **🔤 THREE "VERIFY AGAINST THE RUNNING SYSTEM, NOT A PROXY" TRAPS (E9.61, 2026-08-10):** (a) ⭐ **TWO RENDERERS OF ONE FIELD ARE TWO RULE SETS — a grep clears only the FILE YOU GREPPED.** "MacK Hollins is in the data, not our bug" came from a grep that found no `Mac`-rule — but it grepped the TRACK-RECORD exporter's regex (which genuinely can't match "Mack"), while the BOARD exporter carried a SECOND rule pass upper-casing the letter after any "Mac". "There is no rule that does this" is only true of the file you grepped (sibling of INC-27 grep-the-whole-repo / NF-C0e wrong-key). RULE: any new surface rendering a shared field (e.g. a player name) uses the ONE shared module (`player_naming`), never its own pass; and a "we don't produce this" claim must grep EVERY renderer of that field. (b) **COUNTING BY EYEBALLING A PATTERN CANNOT SEE THE CASES THAT DON'T MATCH IT** — "857/858 names correct" was really 825/858, because scanning for internal capitals structurally can't see `Ceedee Lamb`/`Dj Moore`/`Sam Laporta`. Count by JOINING TO THE AUTHORITY BY ID (nflverse roster), never by scanning for the defect's surface form. (c) **A DEFECT INVISIBLE TO CI NEEDED A LIVE-PAYLOAD RUN** — a fabricated 'worth less' column (the two delta sides built differently: API 1dp-rounded vs browser-recomputed → phantom deltas up to 1.70) was invisible because the two E2E fixture boards are on INCOMPATIBLE point scales, so any magnitude assertion in that harness is meaningless (structure-only). Run the shipped client against LIVE payloads for any magnitude/precision claim. PROCESS SIBLING: two of this story's four items were STALE at pickup — item 3 was already shipped by an earlier build; item 4's card premise ("the frame doesn't carry nflverse display_name") was FALSE (we ingest it; the defect was one hop downstream). RULE: pre-flight a card against the RUNNING SYSTEM before starting — a card written more than a few days before pickup can carry a false premise.
- **🪆 A NESTED MODEL FORM CAN COLLAPSE ONTO ITS OWN FOIL AT A PARAMETER FLOOR — a ~1e-6 CRPS 'lead' then classifies as a SPURIOUS WIN (and a misleading DSR_UNREACHABLE), so a matched nested-vs-foil pair must DETECT the collapse and refuse to count it (MLB Batter Props Ph2, 2026-08-14):** the bake-off's `glm_nb` (Poisson mean + fitted NB2 dispersion) is a strict SUPERSET of the `glm_poisson` foil — on hits/HR the fitted dispersion α hits its FLOOR (the data isn't super-Poisson there), collapsing glm_nb exactly onto the foil. The first harness cut let the resulting ~1e-6 numerical CRPS 'lead' classify as a WIN → a misleading DSR_UNREACHABLE on HR. RULE: when a candidate NESTS its foil (NB⊃Poisson, hurdle⊃base, affine⊃constant), a near-zero margin is a TIE, not a win — add a `tie_with_foil` guard that names the collapse and REFUSES to count it (NF1.8's 'a rank statistic can't tell a tie from a win', at the numerical-precision limit). Reusable for any count-target bake-off with nested forms. (TB, by contrast, was genuinely super-Poisson — the matched pair put +0.036-0.043 CRPS/fold on the dispersion mechanism, a real win.)
- **📐 'WOULD MORE DRAWS CLEAR THIS GATE?' HAS A CHEAP EXACT CEILING — and on any CRN'd bake-off the answer is almost always NO, because the paired delta cancels the draw noise (NF-W7k, 2026-08-18):** to decide whether a higher Monte-Carlo draw count could clear a DSR/deflation gate, do NOT fund the high-draw run — re-score the SAME folds at ≥2 draw SEEDS × ≥2 draw counts, split each per-fold delta's variance into σ²_MC (across-seed, within a fold) vs σ²_het (the rest), and evaluate the gate at the LIMIT σ²_MC=0 — a ceiling no draw count can beat. QB's ceiling: MC was 0.31% of the winner's per-fold variance (a clearing ceiling needed ≥99.8% — 325× short); Sharpe moved 1.013→1.031 at ∞ draws against a 5.513 bar. 48 min vs a high-draw run that answers nothing the ceiling doesn't. ⭐ THE MECHANISM generalises: with COMMON RANDOM NUMBERS every arm transforms the same base normals within a (fold, seed), so the PAIRED delta cancels almost all draw noise before it reaches the metric — the per-fold spread is genuine fold-to-fold heterogeneity, not MC. ⇒ on ANY CRN'd bake-off, 'more draws' is NOT a variance lever without MEASURING first; and 'the draw lever is closed' is a DETERMINISTIC result (no n, no season, no re-run overturns it) → publish NO re-test trigger (NF-D18). ⚠️ SCOPE it honestly: this closes the DRAW lever, NOT the ROW-count or sharper-METRIC levers (untested, not refuted — and the row lever isn't free: more rows/fold = fewer folds, and fold count enters DSR via √(n−1)). Instrument = `fp_mc_variance.py` (decompose → project to the limit → gate on the ceiling). ⭐ SIBLING (a glue proof over the LIVE path found a bug 38 green unit tests missed): when σ²_het≤0 the ceiling sd is 0 and the `sd>1e-12 else 0.0` idiom reported Sharpe 0.0 — turning the MOST favourable case for the lever into the LEAST, on exactly the folds where it's most alive; and a 2-df pre-flight probe's wild G2 ratio (0.37–21.5) is UNINFORMATIVE not adverse (a true ratio of 4 spans [0.21,76] at 2 df) — don't misread an underpowered pre-flight as the extrapolation failing.
- **🔎 A 'DOES X REACH THE SERVING PLANE?' AUDIT NEEDS TWO LEGS — an import-closure walk is BLIND to a filename/parquet consumer (INC-27, read side); and a fail-CLOSED gate is easy to write fail-OPEN (NF-W7j, 2026-08-18):** NF-W7j had to establish whether a degraded research cell (NF-W6d weekly per-stat) reaches any SERVED surface before relaxing a gate on it. RULES: (a) audit with BOTH an IMPORT-CLOSURE leg (walk the serving entry points' import graph) AND a raw FILENAME/parquet-read leg — an import walk cannot see a consumer that reads the parquet by filename with no import edge (INC-27, facing the READ side); each leg carries a POSITIVE CONTROL that RAISES (not passes) when it can't discriminate; (b) make the audit MECHANICAL + FAIL-CLOSED — re-run it every invocation + revert to the strict rule if it stops passing, so if a later story wires the cells into a served surface the hard gate RE-ARMS automatically, nobody has to remember. ⚠️ THE FAIL-CLOSED TRAP: `refuses = audit_ok and …` READS like a precondition but does the OPPOSITE — with the audit FAILING it returns 'does not refuse', so a broken/expired licence silently REMOVES the gate; 'fail closed' means the RAW rule GOVERNS, not 'always refuse' — pin it two-sided. ⭐ SIBLING (a conjunction clause that internally RE-TESTS another makes its isolating guard VACUOUS — NF-D17 refined): `material := effect_well_defined AND point≥band` had clause C re-testing clause D, so deleting D from the conjunction left D's isolating fixture GREEN (NF-D17's own lesson INSIDE the guard written to honour it). An isolating fixture per clause is necessary but NOT sufficient when one clause re-tests another — RED-prove each clause's deletion + check no clause subsumes another. ⭐ SIBLING (governance): do NOT relax the CERTIFICATION BAR for one position after its gate fails — the brief's QB criterion OMITTED dsr_ok, but certifying QB on a dsr_ok-omitting bar AFTER seeing dsr_ok fail is the E2.1-r inversion AND holds QB to a weaker bar than WR/TE/RB were; hold the full gate and hand the decision back (a consumer-vs-shipper certification question is registered FORWARD in the consumer's story, never resolved by quietly dropping a clause).
- **✂️ THE DSR FIELD-TRIM CAN DELETE THE ARM UNDER TEST — `V` is a SAMPLE VARIANCE, so 'drop the most extreme trial Sharpe, re-read DSR' (which this §0.5 canon PRESCRIBES) can drop the WINNER itself and print a near-bar number as a 'coherent sub-field' (NF-W7h, 2026-08-18):** RB's DSR 2×2 trimmed the trial Sharpe furthest from the mean — but with SRs [−6.24, 2.768, −10.25, −6.70] (mean −5.11) that IS the winner (2.768); the trim collapsed `V` 6.4× and moved DSR 0.0→0.909, and the record called the remainder a 'coherent sub-field' — an invitation to register a 3-arm family that CANNOT EXIST (the exact MH2.2 laundering the diagnostic exists to forbid). RULE: before trimming a field to re-read DSR, assert the dropped arm is NOT the winner / arm-under-test; a DSR reached only by deleting the winner is INADMISSIBLE — refuse the multiplicity reading outright and state the variance verdict A FORTIORI ('even deleting `V`'s single largest contributor, a deletion no registration could make, the bar is not reached'). This is a CORRECTION to the DSR-CONV / MH2.5 'exclude the far-out arm' prescription — the exclusion is NON-MONOTONE and can drop the winner; audit every leg that computes the 2×2. ⭐ SIBLING (a verdict rule whose CONJUNCT is TRUE IN ADVANCE passes a no-op): RB's assembled PIT already cleared (0.0242) BEFORE the story, so NF-W7f's ported rule ('cap lifted AND some arm's PIT clears') would have returned PASS at RB for a mechanism that did nothing (2nd conjunct pre-satisfied). When porting a verdict rule to a new position/population, check each conjunct isn't already satisfied there, add a STATE the old rule can't express (RB_CALIBRATION_DAMAGED), and guard that the ported rule and the original DISAGREE on the new data (the vacuous-guard family, at the verdict level). ⭐ SIBLING (architecture — the QB flip does NOT generalize): NF-W7f showed QB's direct-points GENUINE_ABSENCE was a marginal-calibration artifact that FLIPPED after recal; the natural inference was RB flips too. It does NOT — direct points still wins RB by +0.0263 CRPS after recalibration, and RB's headroom is in the DIRECT-POINTS form (oracle 1.4933 vs assembly 2.2403). ⇒ assembly-from-parts wins WR/TE, is competitive at QB, and GENUINELY LOSES at RB → a single architecture across 4 positions is NOT supported; the HYBRID (assemble where it wins, direct-points where it doesn't; boundary at RB) is the better-evidenced default. ⚠️ NF-W8's optimizer needs all 4 positions on ONE generator + one level recal — so a green RB gate does NOT unblock it, and the hybrid ADDS a cross-position-comparability problem; 'RB pays' is not progress toward NF-W8.
- **🚫 A DIAGNOSTIC DSR DOES NOT TRANSFER TO A NARROWER FIELD — DECLARE THE MECHANISM-JUSTIFIED FAMILY *BEFORE* COMPUTING ANY PER-FAMILY DSR, OR THE FAMILY CHOICE IS LAUNDERED (NF-INJ3 → NF-INJ3b, 2026-08-22/23; the missing half of the 'fresh coherent re-registration' prescription):** the canon above says a DSR failure over a heterogeneous field is a hypothesis about the FIELD, and the remedy is a FRESH registration with a coherent family. What it did not say is how the successor's family may be CHOSEN. `V` is a SAMPLE VARIANCE over the field's trial Sharpes, so a diagnostic DSR computed with some arm removed is **specific to that exact membership and moves NON-MONOTONICALLY** with it — DSR-CONV already documents that dropping a NEAR-MEAN arm WIDENS `V` and RAISES the bar. ⇒ **a narrower successor family does NOT inherit the parent's diagnostic figure, and must not be registered as though it does.** ⭐ THE OPERATIONAL RULE, and it constrains the PARENT story as much as the successor: **do not compute a menu of per-candidate-family DSRs.** Publishing 'family A → 0.94, family B → 0.97, family C → 0.91' hands the successor a family selected on its DSR — the MH2.2 laundering in a successor's badge, one hop removed. NF-INJ3 deliberately computed NONE and said so in its own record; NF-INJ3b's registration therefore declares its family on MECHANISM first and computes DSR only after. A single diagnostic that varies ONE convention the program already owns (here MH2.1 (a): the incumbent REFERENCE arm's identically-zero trial Sharpe ∉ `V`) is admissible to REPORT — it names what separates the null from a ship (NF-W7h) — but it is a diagnostic about the PARENT's registration, never a bar the successor inherits. ⭐ SIBLING (the parent defect worth stating on its own): **a pre-registration must name its DEFLATION CONVENTIONS, not just its arms, folds, metric and thresholds.** NF-INJ3 declared nine gates, a matched foil, four anchors and a per-form oracle — and returned a null on two details it never wrote down: `V`'s MEMBERSHIP and the BH FAMILY. Those are exactly the specification items that only become interesting AFTER a result, i.e. the ones you can no longer set (E2.1-r). Name them up front: which arms are in `V` (degenerates ∉ `V` per DSR-CONV **and** any reference/incumbent arm ∉ `V` per MH2.1 (a) — a structural 0.0 inflates a small family's `V` exactly as a diagnostic anchor does; measured 4.8× on five non-degenerate arms, ~0.082 of DSR), and what the BH family IS (one mechanism × one population with no registered position axis is arguably a SINGLE hypothesis, and correcting across ARMS deflates a second time for the search DSR already deflates — say which reading binds, and why, before scoring).
- **🔁 'ASK WHAT TRANSFERS' CAN MEAN THE *FINDING*, NOT THE FEATURE — a certified study whose FEATURES are structurally unusable can still hand a new story its best-performing mechanism (NF-W2 → NF-INJ3, 2026-08-22):** NF-INJ3 opened by asking what the certified weekly injury family (NF-W2/W2b/W2d) could contribute to a SEASON-level target. Its FEATURES could not transfer at ALL — the weekly source has no preseason rows and no 2026 rows, so not one covariate was obtainable. Its measured FINDING transferred cleanly: *the lift lives in the zero/availability leg*, re-expressed as an explicit hurdle `P(plays ≥ 1) × E[games | plays]`, which **won the field** (+0.2845 CRPS, 6/7 folds) and beat the story's own headline hypothesis by ~2× on the channel decomposition (hurdle split +0.0472, p 0.0555 vs timing +0.0252, p 0.098). ⇒ **when a prior study's data cannot be reused, do not close the transfer question — re-ask it about the MECHANISM.** A certified finding is a portable hypothesis about WHERE the signal lives; a certified feature set is not portable at all once the feed changes. And carry the corollary honestly: a story whose registered headline hypothesis lands SMALLEST of its live channels reports that plainly (NF-INJ3's timing proxy), rather than letting a transferred mechanism's win read as confirmation of the hypothesis it was registered to test.
- **🗓️ A COMMITTED ABLATION ARTIFACT MUST BE REGENERATED FROM THE COMMITTED CODE THAT CLAIMS TO HAVE PRODUCED IT — the generating code's commit must be the SAME AS OR AN ANCESTOR OF the artifact's commit (PM rule, NF-INJ3b D3, 2026-08-23):** a §0.5 record's whole authority is that the numbers in it came from the code beside it. NF-INJ3's committed SMOKE artifact was generated **eleven minutes BEFORE the module's first commit** — a pre-commit DEVELOPMENT vintage — and it no longer reproduces (measured 1.02e-2 on exactly the three L-BFGS-B-fitted arms), while the same story's FULL artifact reproduces at **exactly 0.0** in the same environment. ⭐ THE TELL, and it is cheap: **only the ITERATIVELY-FITTED arms diverge.** A closed-form or constant arm is bit-stable across environments and code churn, so a diff that lands on precisely the optimiser-fitted arms is a VINTAGE/ENVIRONMENT difference, not a data or logic change — and the discriminating control is to re-run the PARENT'S OWN ENTRYPOINT in the current environment and diff that against its recorded artifact (if the re-run matches YOUR run but not the RECORD, the record is the stale thing). ⚠️ Nothing turned on it there — a smoke is a code-path proof and never a gate — but the failure mode is a record whose numbers cannot be reproduced from the code it ships with, which is exactly what a later reader trusts it for. ⇒ **REGENERATE an ablation artifact from the committed code before committing it** (in practice: make the code commit first, or regenerate immediately before staging), and when a stale one is found, do NOT silently refresh it — date the onset (artifact `generated_at` vs `git log` on the generating module) and record the finding, because a mid-development artifact that outran its code is a PROVENANCE defect worth naming, not a diff to tidy away.
- **🪣 NEVER BUCKET A DECOMPOSITION BY QUANTILES ON A BIMODAL COVARIATE — equal-count buckets lump a near-empty middle with a dense tail and FABRICATE a monotone gradient; and a PRE-COMMITTED headline off a 1-fold smoke can be REFUTED by the decisive run (NF-W7f, 2026-08-17):** the decision to run pre-committed the record's causal headline ('passing_yards prices availability TWICE, sign-flips with P(played)') off a ONE-FOLD smoke that bucketed the availability decomposition by π̂ QUARTILES (equal counts). QB's π̂ is BIMODAL (~390 rows <0.4, ~250 >0.9, almost nothing between), so quartiles lumped the near-empty middle with the dense tail and manufactured a tidy monotone gradient. The decisive 8-fold run — re-bucketed on FIXED ABSOLUTE EDGES with raw Σsums/Σcounts pooled and all 10 buckets evaluable — found the decomposition NON-MONOTONE (6 sign changes; the two buckets holding 57% of rows at −0.16/−0.03; every large swing in a 102–176-row bucket), and the row-blind MATCHED FOIL (identical machinery, availability content STRIPPED) LOST by +1.187 CRPS 8/8 → removing availability content is CATASTROPHIC, so 'double-pricing' is dead as a mechanism. ⭐ RULES: (a) decompose on FIXED absolute edges + pooled raw sums, never per-fold/quantile buckets (the NF1.8 mean-of-means + this bimodal-gradient class); (b) a headline pre-committed off a smoke is kept VERBATIM under a SUPERSEDED marker when the decisive run overturns it — a pre-registration edited after its result is no longer one, and the whole value of recording a smoke diagnosis is that the run can refute it; propagate the correction to every card/doc quoting the old clause. ⭐ SIBLING (architecture) — **a `beats_foil` architecture verdict can be an ARTIFACT of an UNCALIBRATED marginal layer:** NF-W7c filed QB `GENUINE_ABSENCE` vs the direct-points foil ('assembly-from-parts loses at QB/RB' → the roadmap's 'maybe hybrid' question), but after the zero-mass recal the assembled QB BEATS that foil (2.5645 vs 2.5834). Recalibrate the marginal layer before concluding an architecture loses; the hybrid question re-opens, RB untested under the same fix. ⭐ SIBLING (substrate) — a continuous per-stat cell can UNDER-PRICE its own zero atom (QB|passing_yards predicted P(0)=0.33 vs realized 0.556) — the named QB defect, plausibly also rushing/receiving_yards → the most direct untested lead on RB (the other NF-W8 blocker). ⭐ SIBLING (reporting) — a BINDING/activity SHARE is invariant to the MAGNITUDE it binds at (the clamp's share read 0.917 before AND after while its distortion fell 112×, 0.253→0.002); report the magnitude BESIDE the share or 'nothing changed' hides a constraint that STOPPED MATTERING (NF-D20 one level over).
- **🧩 TWO MECHANISM HALVES THAT EACH MOVE A METRIC ARE NOT ADDITIVE — measure the full 2×2 on COMMON RANDOM NUMBERS; and a knob that IMPROVES the proper score while WORSENING calibration is pricing the effect TWICE (NF-W7e, 2026-08-17; confirms NF-W7d's bundle + atom-cap):** NF-W7d's availability mixture bundled two changes (a split + a played-rows-only Σ) that netted a QB null; NF-W7e ran the full 2×2 (split on/off × Σ_all/Σ_played) on CRN and found the halves NON-ADDITIVE — the split is worth ~HALF over Σ_all what it was over Σ_played (ratios 0.43–0.59), and once the split is on the better Σ POPULATION is POSITION-SPECIFIC (Σ_all at QB/WR, Σ_played at RB, TE a tie). ⇒ ⛔ never recombine two channels from separate CONDITIONAL measurements (the NF-W7d 'split+all-rows Σ was measured over the played Σ' warning, now measured) — register the recombination forward + run the 2×2. ⭐ THE TELL that a knob prices an effect TWICE: at QB the all-rows Σ IMPROVED CRPS (+0.0064) while MOVING PIT THE WRONG WAY (0.0595→0.0648) — a proper-score gain bought by a calibration loss means the joint layer is re-pricing something a lower layer already carries (here availability). ⭐ ATOM-CAP CONFIRMED (was an NF-W7d inference): QB's installed zero-atom is Σ-INVARIANT to the last digit; the per-stat marginals admit at most 0.269 atom vs a realized 0.516 all-zero rate; the best QB PIT any joint arm can post is ~0.06 → QB is BLOCKED AT THE MARGINAL LAYER, NO joint-layer story can calibrate it. This CLOSES the joint-layer QB line — the lever is the 52-cell per-stat SUBSTRATE (the QB cells that under-price the atom). NF-W7e certified WR (first assembled distribution at WR; TE was NF-W7c) — both deploy-held.
- **🧬 A MATCHED FOIL DECOMPOSES A *BUNDLED* NULL INTO OPPOSITELY-SIGNED CHANNELS — the bundled number LIES, and without the per-channel foil the record would say 'the mechanism is inert' (NF-W7d, 2026-08-16; the NF-D10/D15 matched-foil family, now separating a NULL into two mechanisms):** NF-W7d's QB availability-mixture arm changed TWO things at once (an explicit availability SPLIT + estimating Σ on played rows only) and netted −0.0031 CRPS = GENUINE_ABSENCE. The matched foil (the mixture's own machinery with the availability term OFF) separated the channels: the availability SPLIT pays at ALL FOUR positions (+0.0036 to +0.0161, positive 4/4) while the played-rows-only Σ COSTS at all four (−0.0015 to −0.0180); QB nets negative ONLY because its Σ penalty is ~4× the others (QB has by far the biggest marginal-vs-conditional dependence gap). ⇒ a bundled null is not a verdict on either half — when a registered arm bundles ≥2 changes, a matched foil PER CHANNEL is what recovers the real (here oppositely-signed) mechanism; the +0.0149 split was invisible inside the −0.0031 bundle. ⛔ but a channel measured CONDITIONAL on the other half's setting (the split's +0.0149 was measured over the played-rows Σ) is NOT a measurement of 'split + all-rows Σ' — asserting the recombination will win is post-hoc field construction (MH2.2); it must be REGISTERED FORWARD. ⭐ SIBLING — **a calibration defect can be BLOCKED AT A LOWER LAYER than the one you're modelling: a joint-layer story can only price the atom the MARGINAL layer already carries.** QB's realized all-zero rate is 0.516 and π̂ wants 0.486 (right size), but the marginal-admissibility constraint binds on 91.7% of rows so only 0.267 of the atom installs (~half) → the atom cap depends only on the per-stat distributions + π, NOT the correlation choice, so no JOINT-layer story can clear QB's PIT — it's a MARGINAL-layer (52-cell substrate) problem. Confirm which layer a defect lives at before modelling the upper one. ⭐ SIBLING (guard) — **a self-validating check that owns its OWN COPY of the scored logic passes silently when the SCORED path breaks:** NF-W7d's marginal-preservation guard validated a re-implementation, so deleting the conditional shift from the actually-scored code left all tests green (the exact double-counting defect the check exists to catch) → both paths now share ONE function (the NF-C0e wired≠invoked / guard-restates-the-code family).
- **🕳️ A CORRECTION DERIVED FROM AN ESTIMATE THE MODEL ALREADY USES IS NOT AUTOMATICALLY "DOUBLE-PRICING" — MEASURE IT WITH A FOIL THAT STRIPS THE DERIVED CONTENT, AND A PER-FOLD *QUANTILE* BUCKETING WILL MANUFACTURE THE GRADIENT YOU EXPECTED (NF-W7f, 2026-08-17; a pre-committed mechanism REFUTED by the captures taken to test it):** NF-W7f recalibrated the QB per-stat cells' zero mass and VINDICATED NF-W7d's marginal-layer diagnosis — atom cap 0.2687→0.5481, installed atom 0.2776→0.5176 vs a realized 0.5162, assembled PIT 0.0648→0.0281 **clearing the 0.05 bar on 8/8 folds where the incumbent clears 0/8**, +0.0184 CRPS over the matched foil (CI [0.0032,0.0336], 6/8, p=0.0121, PBO 0.0, 100% flip mass on the winner) — yet was CONSTRAINT_REFUSED by a component-degradation clause. A one-fold smoke had diagnosed the cause as "the yardage cell already prices availability internally, so an availability-derived zero target prices it TWICE, and the sign flips with P(played)", and that reading was **pre-committed as the record's headline before the decisive run**. Both halves were then refuted by the story's own pre-registered captures. **RULES: (a) ⭐ THE "IT'S ALREADY PRICED INTERNALLY" HYPOTHESIS NEEDS A ROW-BLIND MATCHED FOIL — the identical machinery driven by a POPULATION-level target instead of the row-level estimate. Here that foil LOST by +1.1871 CRPS, 8/8 folds, p=0.0 — the largest positive channel in the story ⇒ removing the derived content is catastrophic, not corrective, and "double-pricing" is dead as a mechanism. (b) ⭐⭐ NEVER BUCKET A DECOMPOSITION BY PER-FOLD QUANTILES: "bucket k" is then a different population on every fold (so no multi-fold pool means anything), it pools a mean-of-means (NF1.8), AND on a BIMODAL covariate equal-COUNT buckets lump a near-empty middle in with a dense tail and FABRICATE a monotone gradient. NF-W7f's quartile view read a tidy sign flip (+0.58/−0.30/−1.95/−0.19); the same data on FIXED absolute edges, pooled over 8 folds as Σsums/Σcounts with all 10 buckets evaluable, is NON_MONOTONE with SIX sign changes, the two buckets holding 57% of rows sit at −0.16/−0.03, and every large swing lives in a 102–176-row bucket. Emit raw SUMS AND COUNTS per fold (never means), report a thin bucket as None and <2 signed buckets as UNDEFINED — never as "no crossover" (NF1.7 (a)).** ⭐ **SIBLING — AN ACTIVITY COUNT IS NOT A MAGNITUDE, and a binding/clamp SHARE is invariant to the level it binds at:** the clamp's binding share read **0.917 before and after** — byte-identical to the predecessor's — while its mean move on π̂ collapsed **0.2527→0.0023 (112×)** and the installed atom nearly doubled. A headline quoting the share alone says *nothing changed* about a constraint that had stopped mattering; report the MAGNITUDE beside any binding/active/hit-rate share (NF-D20 one level over — measure whether the mechanism could act, don't infer it from an activity count). ⭐ **SIBLING — A DSR FAILURE IS *NOT ALWAYS* A FIELD-COMPOSITION STORY: measure the 2×2 before prescribing a coherent re-registration.** The repo now has four cases where a fresh coherent family converted a DSR/constraint refusal into a ship (MARGIN2→3, W7→W7b, W6b-C, NCAAF-S1) and that pattern was being applied as automatic. NF-W7f is the COUNTEREXAMPLE: dropping the row-blind arm cuts cross-trial dispersion **V 8.8× (27.15→3.07)** and DSR reaches only **0.174** against a 0.95 bar, so coherence is NOT the lever — `classify_null`'s own `DSR_UNREACHABLE` reading ("even a 2-arm field does not clear at this fold count and dispersion; the only lever left is a lower-variance design") was RIGHT and the session's field instinct was WRONG. The binding quantity was per-fold noise in the delta (mean 0.0184, sd 0.0182, 2 negative folds) ⇒ the candidate lever is **DRAWS** (Monte-Carlo error in the per-fold scores), registered FORWARD; ⛔ never a post-hoc trim (MH2.2), and ⛔ never "more seasons". ⭐ **SIBLING — A MIXED STATISTICAL+ANCHOR NULL MUST CLASSIFY BY THE BINDING HALF:** when a statistical gate AND a non-rescuable anchor both fail, the anchor BINDS (more folds could clear the statistical half and the ship would still be refused) ⇒ state `CONSTRAINT_REFUSED` with `binding_half`, publish NO fold/season trigger, and REPORT the statistical shortfall + the instrument's own verdict verbatim rather than hiding either (NF-D18's misleading direction, which the default `classify_null` fall-through would have published). And the successor lesson, stated precisely because the obvious reading over-shoots: **the binding cell MOVED** (after recalibration the majority binding cell is a leg already carrying MORE zero mass than its realized rate, which a RAISE-ONLY transform structurally cannot correct) — but that is a CEILING on future headroom, ⛔ NOT the next thing to fix: the cap ends at 0.5481 against an installed atom of 0.5176 with the clamp moving π̂ by 0.0023, i.e. **the cap is no longer binding and the atom already sits at the realized rate**. What actually remains between this arm and a certificate is not a mechanism at all — a FORWARD decision on the component clause and a LOWER-VARIANCE design. ⇒ when a story's constraint moves, re-check whether it still BINDS before carding a successor against it.
- **🏈 A PER-POSITION-CERTIFIED DISTRIBUTION IS SAFE ON A NON-COMPARATIVE SURFACE BUT NOT AS INPUT TO A CROSS-POSITION RANKING — a mixed-generator board shifts the PRIMARY sort key by CODE-OF-ORIGIN (NF-W7c §4, 2026-08-16; the MH2.1 bundle lesson, where the bundle is the BOARD):** NF-W7c certified the assembly at TE only; serving it as a fantasy OPTIMIZER input is unsafe even though certification is genuinely per-position, because the optimizer ranks on VOR (cross-position scarcity) — verified in `fantasy_engine/draft.py`, not assumed: **(a)** the assembly produces its OWN mean, so a systematic level gap between the assembled TE and the incumbent-path RB/WR shifts every TE up/down the board by which CODE produced the number, not by skill — and ⚠️ NF-TR2's level recalibration lives on the INCUMBENT path, so an assembled point would NOT inherit it; **(b)** dispersion becomes generator-dependent (TE gets honest wider tails while RB/WR keep understated ones → false apparent upside on ceiling/boom/vor_p90); **(c)** FLEX compares a TE distribution to a WR's directly. This is MH2.1's 'a one-target swap breaks every consumer that assumes targets move as a bundle' — here the bundle is the BOARD. RULE: a per-position/per-target-certified distribution can ship on a NON-COMPARATIVE surface (a single-player 'what might X do this week' view, with its `calibration_warning`) but must NOT feed a CROSS-POSITION ranking until EVERY compared position is on the same generator AND the same level recalibration. Baseball is the precedent, not the counterexample: `home_win` vs `total_runs` are never ranked against each other so per-target independence is safe there; a fantasy board ranks TE vs RB for the SAME slot, so it isn't.
- **🃏 `classify_null` NAMES THE FOIL, NOT THE HYPOTHESIS — a GENUINE_ABSENCE against a DIRECT-POINTS foil answers 'does assembly-from-parts beat modelling the total DIRECTLY?', NOT 'does the mechanism help?', and reading it as the latter is exactly BACKWARDS (NF-W7c, 2026-08-16; the 6th hand-correction of that classifier):** NF-W7c's per-player fantasy-point assembly returned SHIP at TE, POWER_LIMITED at WR, GENUINE_ABSENCE at QB/RB — but `beats_foil` compares against the BEST foil, which at QB/RB is `foil_direct_points` (a learner pointed straight at league points). So the QB/RB null says 'assembling from per-stat parts does not beat modelling the total directly' — it says NOTHING about the story's mechanism (cross-stat correlation), which PASSES at all four positions (independent draws under-cover 0.74–0.79 vs the 0.80 floor; modelling correlation fixes coverage AND wins the proper score +0.057–0.105 CRPS, not a coverage-for-accuracy trade). 'dependence is dead at QB/RB' would be exactly backwards. RULE: before reading a null as 'the mechanism failed,' check WHICH foil `beats_foil` ran against — a null against a stronger DIFFERENT-FAMILY foil is an ARCHITECTURE verdict, not a mechanism verdict. ⭐ PROGRAM FINDING: assembly-from-parts is NOT universally right — it wins at WR/TE and LOSES to a direct-points learner at QB/RB, the sign split falling at the availability-ratio boundary → the roadmap may need a HYBRID (assemble where it wins, model the total directly where it doesn't), not one architecture everywhere. ⭐ SIBLING — **a VIOLATION/refusal gate must require SIGNIFICANT *AND* MATERIAL, not significance alone (NF-W6's demonstrable≠material, applied to an oracle-floor):** NF-W7c's first cut gated an oracle-floor violation on statistical significance alone, and a constant-offset series has ZERO paired variance so it called a 2e-4 gap 'significant' → it would have refused the story at every position. A violation now requires significant AND ≥1/10 of the arm's claimed effect (its OWN fixture caught it — the smoke-finds-what-84-guards-miss class). ⭐ SIBLING — **a SINGLE Gaussian copula cannot simultaneously model an AVAILABILITY atom and within-game STRUCTURE, and the availability RATIO orders the failure while the zero-atom SIZE does not (NF-W7c QB):** QB is the only position failing PIT flatness, in EVERY representation incl. the copula-free direct-points foil (structural to the field). Cause measured: a single copula fits one compromise Σ between an availability factor and a conditional-on-playing structure, with ZERO tail dependence by construction, so at ρ̂≈0.24 it can't reproduce a 53.9% joint-zero atom. The RATIO (all-rows ρ̄ / played-only ρ̄: QB 1.88× vs RB 1.32× / WR 1.23× / TE 1.14×) orders the PIT failure; the zero-atom SIZE does NOT (RB has the largest joint-zero excess, 17.6×, and passes). Fix = an explicit availability MIXTURE (Bernoulli(plays) × conditional-on-playing joint, Σ on played rows only) — ⚠️ but NF-W4 already nulled an availability mixture ×4 (per-stat), so it's not a free win; NF-W7c gives a sharper measured target (the assembled total's joint-zero atom). Applies to any zero-heavy JOINT draw (DST, same-game legs, stacks).
- **🎲 ESTIMATE A COUNT-LEG COPULA ON RAW RANKS, NOT MODEL-RESIDUAL PITs — randomized-PIT z-scores ATTENUATE ρ̂ on zero-heavy discrete margins (NF-W7b, 2026-08-14):** NF-W7b's `joint_double` (×2 attenuation probe, registered as a REAL arm per NF-D20 so an under-correcting estimator can't hide behind an anchor's ineligibility) BEAT the model-residual-PIT estimator `joint_rankcorr` → confirmed randomized-PIT z-scores on zero-heavy discrete margins (sacks/INT/TD/PA-tier) systematically UNDER-estimate the dependence. RULE: for any copula/dependence fit over count or zero-heavy legs, estimate ρ on RAW OUTCOME RANKS (Spearman → 2·sin(πρ/6)), not on model-residual PITs. SIBLINGS (all reusable for NF-W8/W10): (a) DEPENDENCE DIRECTION matters far more than its magnitude — the 4 arms sat in a 0.12% contender band with 87% flip-mass on the winner, so get the SIGN structure right, don't over-tune ρ; (b) fixing under-dispersion was a FREE CRPS WIN (+0.0273 vs the refused indep arm), not a coverage-for-score trade — a correlated sum drawn independently hurts the proper score too, not just coverage; (c) `joint_draw.py` is the reusable substrate (Gaussian copula, PSD repair, one-factor loadings, comonotone with per-leg direction flips, estimation floors that REFUSE below 50 rows rather than silently defaulting to independence, + 3 dependence gate clauses: incumbent_refusal_reproduces / dependence_moves_coverage / beats_indep_on_coverage). ⭐ SECOND consecutive CONSTRAINT_REFUSED→registered-successor→SHIP (after MARGIN2→3): an NF-D18 refusal points at the answer, the successor targets the refused quantity under a FRESH registration, and the anchors (not judgment) confirm the mechanism.
- **🎯 INTERSECT A CORRECTED FEATURE WITH THE SERVED CONTRACT BEFORE REFLEXIVELY RETRAINING — and a 'BOUNDED-BY-EXPOSURE' NULL IS FINAL WITH NO RE-TEST TRIGGER (E1.13, 2026-08-14):** three shipped data corrections (E9.48 injury 42,416 rows, E9.53 seasonnorm, E11.24-6c matchup-σ) changed the HISTORY of features models might have trained on (the E7.9 train/serve-mismatch class). The disciplined first move is NOT to retrain — it's to INTERSECT each corrected feature with the SERVED CHAMPION CONTRACTS (the exact column union the served pickles consume). Two of three reached NO served contract → recorded NULL, no retrain (the corruption lived entirely in features the champions don't consume — ⭐ and trace INDIRECT routes explicitly: a sub-model may TRAIN on the feature while NO serving read consumes its signal — offense sub-models train on injury features but predict_today reads only bullpen_mu_v2). ⭐ For the one correction that DID reach a contract, the §0.5 retrain was INCUMBENT_STANDS as a NEW null shape: a **BOUNDED-BY-EXPOSURE null** — the refit genuinely WINS on the touched rows (+0.086 CRPS on 71 rows) but the correction's ~1.5% EXPOSURE caps the pooled margin at ~0.0013 (sub-noise) BY CONSTRUCTION. ⛔ This gets NO re-test trigger — unlike a POWER-LIMITED null, no fold count or window can move the exposure SHARE (the fraction of rows the correction touches is structural), so publishing a 'retest with more data' trigger would be the NF-D18 misleading direction. The ONLY legitimate reopen is a change in the EXPOSURE itself (a new outage class making the corrected-row condition common), which the coverage guards surface first.
- **🎯 A SCORE-CONTEST WIN THAT FAILS A COVERAGE FLOOR IS A DEPENDENCE DEFECT, NOT A SIGNAL DEFECT — and it is DISTINCT from tail-truncation (NF-W7, 2026-08-13):** NF-W7's assembled DST distribution BEAT all three foils on CRPS (+0.0338, CI excludes 0, DSR 0.961) yet FAILED the pre-registered cov(80) floor (0.7603 vs 0.80, ~4.6 SE) → CONSTRAINT_REFUSED. Cause: the component banks were drawn INDEPENDENTLY, but DST legs (sacks/INT/FR/PA-bucket) CO-MOVE, so the assembled sum UNDER-DISPERSES. ⭐ DIFFERENT mechanism from NF-MARGIN1 tail-truncation — a correct tail model (inherited + working here: K cov 0.867) does NOT fix missing correlation. RULE: for any assembly of correlated components (DST, same-game legs, team totals, stacks) independent draws are a MEASURED, REFUSED simplification, not a benign default — model cross-component dependence (a joint/copula draw); a win on the point-score WITH a coverage-floor refusal names DEPENDENCE as the remedy, never more signal. SIBLINGS (same story): (a) ⭐ a banned-TOKEN / keyword SQL scan that is a RAW SUBSTRING match false-fires on `'temp' ⊂ 'attempt'` — a kicker story literally cannot say 'attempt' under it; use an IDENTIFIER-BOUNDARY scan (word boundaries), not substring (the over-eager-guard class); (b) `schedules` carries ERA franchise codes (OAK/SD) while pbp/rosters carry CURRENT — an uncanonicalized inner join (a bye-filter here) silently drops era-team rows (the NF-W3 franchise-code family, now on the schedule side); (c) a COVERAGE-FLOOR-ONLY refusal (every other gate green) must classify CONSTRAINT_REFUSED (NF-D18), ⛔ NEVER POWER_LIMITED — the latter publishes a fold-count re-test trigger for a shortfall no fold count can move, plus a self-contradicting 'interval spans zero' reason about a CI that EXCLUDES zero (5th hand-correction of the vertical's null classifier → the carded classify_null fix inherits this shape).
- **📐 WHEN A §0.5 EVALUATION FAMILY IS A PER-ROW-ANCHORED SHARED OFFSET, THE CRPS/PINBALL OPTIMUM *IS* THE CALIBRATION CONDITION — the direct empirical estimator targets the refuted quantity with nothing to mis-specify, and a form fit (GPD/parametric) is the exact bias class to avoid (NF-MARGIN3, 2026-08-13):** for a shared per-position offset added above each row's own grid end, the pooled pinball optimum at level u is exactly the offset where the calibration EXCEEDANCE RATE = 1−u. ⇒ 'calibrated on eval-end exceedances' and 'metric-optimal' are the SAME estimator — no bake-off between them is needed, and it estimates the refuted quantity DIRECTLY. ⛔ A GPD/tail-index/parametric fit is a LIKELIHOOD PROXY for the CRPS optimum = precisely the mis-specification the anchors refuse; its shape param is unstable at the ~40-80 exceedances a fold-cell carries; and parametric extrapolation only pays BEYOND the grid, where the metric can't see it. RULE: before reaching for a distributional form, check whether the eval family makes the metric optimum a direct calibration condition — if so, fit that condition, don't fit a form. SIBLING (the CONSTRAINT_REFUSED remedy, WORKED end-to-end): when ANCHORS (not statistics) refuse an arm — `over_ext` ×3 registered-to-lose BEATS it, a permutation edges it — the fix is a SUCCESSOR estimator targeting the refuted quantity under a FRESH registration (⛔ never a re-run, never a post-hoc field shrink); MARGIN2→MARGIN3 converted 2 refusals into 2 ships, and the anchors' OWN behavior flipping (over_ext now loses, permutation now loses OUTRIGHT p=1.0) is the mechanism confirmation — a permuted/inflated anchor only ever won because the base estimator UNDER-extended.
- **📉 A SHRINKAGE MODEL WITH MISSING COVARIATES FAILS BY COMPRESSING TOWARD THE MEAN, NOT BY MIS-RANKING — QA a thin/pre-season board on its SPREAD, never on who's on top (NCAAF-PS, 2026-08-18):** the P0.7 note propagated 'the cold-start board shows Indiana spuriously LEADING' as the pre-season tell — MEASURED WRONG: Indiana still leads after a full re-fit; what the missing fall-camp covariates actually did was SHRINK every margin toward the mean (OSU vs Ball St +19.7→+40.4; slate P(home win) span 0.356-0.883 → 0.117-0.992 after the re-fit; interval widths UNCHANGED because σ comes from the served dispersion artifact, not the strengths). RULE: a partial-pooling / EB-shrunk model starved of covariates regresses toward the pooled mean — the symptom is a COMPRESSED distribution (a flat board), NOT a wrong ranking. QA it on the SPREAD of the predictions (margin range, P(win) span), never on the leader; 'an unexpected leader' is not an alarm, 'a flat board' is. ⭐ SIBLING (a re-fit that reads a stale INTERMEDIATE silently reproduces the cold start AND reports success — the INC-25 / run-vs-worked class): the NCAAF P1.2 re-fit reads covariates from the DuckDB MARTS, not the lake, so `run_team_strength --s3` against stale marts reproduces the cold-start default and returns SUCCESS (happened 08-17). The discriminator between 'it ran' and 'it worked' is the OUTPUT covariate being non-zero (0/0/138 = cold start; 136/136/138 = a real re-fit) — verify the covariate, not the exit code; rebuild the marts from the fresh lake FIRST.
- **🏟️ SIX REUSABLE FINDINGS FROM NCAAF-P2.1 (2026-08-15; a 16-arm pre-registered structural battery → REFERENCE_STANDS):** (a) ⭐ **PBO AND DSR WANT DIFFERENT RETURN SERIES — sharing ONE silently taxes DSR.** CSCV/PBO needs MANY buckets; DSR needs LOW-NOISE INDEPENDENT observations. The one real effect (`pace`, +0.062 CRPS, 8/8 folds, p=0.002, clean PBO 0.023) failed DSR (0.041) partly because its per-BUCKET Sharpe (0.532) is ~3× below its per-FOLD Sharpe (1.492) — same effect, same folds, the gap is the SERIES DEFINITION alone. ⇒ pre-register the DSR return series SEPARATELY from the PBO bucket series; ⛔ never re-read the pre-registered gate on the better-looking series (the E2.1-r inversion). (b) ⭐ **VERIFY EACH HYPOTHESIS AGAINST THE MODEL CODE, NOT THE SPEC — 6 of the battery's premises were WRONG on contact:** the shipped model had NO home-field term at all (the intercept absorbs one bump blended over 7.93% neutral-site rows), H2's 'runs on net strength' premise was false (off/def are already linear terms; the real gap is the interaction), `is_postseason` was in `_ID_COLS` (no config could condition on bowls), garbage-time was SCORE-MARGIN not WP gated, weather isn't in the lake at all (a hypothesis that can't be measured must not spend multiplicity), only 4 of 9 doc matchup interactions existed. A spec-driven battery MUST code-verify first, and RECORD the corrections rather than silently absorb them. (c) ⭐ **A DSR_UNREACHABLE null gets NO re-test trigger, and a smaller field is NOT always the remedy — `classify_null` OVERTURNED the session's own arithmetic:** the operator had computed a ~2-arm field might clear and was about to recommend a narrow-family successor; `classify_null(declared_field_size=16, degenerates_excluded_from_v=True)` reported `max_field_size=0` and was right — n enters DSR only through √(n−1), so it scales a positive gap but can't create one (the MH2.7 instrument working as designed — trust the machine flag over your own arithmetic). (d) **A SILENTLY-WRONG FEATURE caught only by median-vs-known-magnitude:** CFBD `yardsGained` on a punt is the RETURN yardage, not punt distance → a 'punt average' of 1.27 yards, finite + well-typed + wrong, no error anywhere; caught by comparing the median to the real-world ~42. Sanity-check a derived feature's median against its known magnitude. (e) **A TARGET-ENCODED feature needs OUT-OF-FOLD encoding or its in-fold collapse is an ESTIMATOR ARTIFACT, not a verdict:** the EB per-team HFA arm (built from the label on the fold's train rows, used as a predictor on those same rows) collapsed 0/8 — textbook target-encoding overfit; the matched constant foil is structurally immune, so the comparison is CONFOUNDED and per-team HFA is 'not cleanly refuted' (needs nested-CV OOF encoding). (f) **A POOLED metric DILUTES an effect whose mechanism touches few rows** — venue/travel HFA can only move the 7.93% neutral-site + travel/altitude extremes, so a pooled CRPS over all games washes it toward zero; the honest successor registers a subset metric forward (the bounded-by-exposure family, E1.13). Plus two harness lessons: a non-promoted SURVIVOR must still be null-classified (the harness left `pace` with no null state — the exact line between 'buy more seasons' and 'no n ever clears'; fixed + guarded), and the first coverage-floor guard tested the clause fns while the defect lived at the CALL SITE (vacuous; the RED-proof caught it).
- **📊 A MARGIN-ATTRIBUTION SHARE OVER A SUB-NOISE DENOMINATOR IS NOISE AMPLIFICATION, NOT A PROPORTION — cite the ABSOLUTE component; and a learner_share>1 is a SIGN FLIP, not over-crediting (MH1, 2026-08-18):** every §0.5 bake-off headline margin = incumbent − leader ARM (arm = contract×learner) blends TWO effects — what the FEATURES bought (contract) vs what the LEARNER-CLASS swap bought (learner_swap = incumbent − the incumbent contract under the LEADER's learner; contract = that reference − leader; sum == total exactly). The shared owner is `betting_ml/utils/margin_attribution.py`; every report now emits the split (an INACTIVE decomposition — a single-contract run with no contract axis — carries a NAMED reason, never silence: "we checked, it came back clean" must not look like "we didn't check"). TWO readings the raw share can't give: (a) a SUB-NOISE denominator — a share is a ratio, and a ratio whose denominator sits inside the metric's own noise floor (0.02 crps) is noise amplification; the % is computed but must NOT be headlined — cite the ABSOLUTE contract component (E7.9's widely-quoted '54%'/'77%' are sub-noise ratios of 0.0053/0.0127-crps margins → cite +0.0059/+0.0012; only its '74%' clears the floor); (b) learner_share>1 = a SIGN FLIP — the contract component points OPPOSITE the headline, so holding the learner fixed the 'winning' contract LOST (a categorically different error from over-crediting: the served 14-col morning-totals contract is −0.0029 WORSE learner-fixed → its serve was a PBO-fix (0.543→0.054) + ~6× leanness win at NO detectable accuracy cost, NEVER an accuracy improvement — but NOT a rollback: bet_paused, best_alpha=0, the promotion gate said HOLD, all sub-noise). ⭐ the exonerating side: home_win/pre_lineup (the program's ONLY PROMOTE) is 11% learner / 89% CONTRACT — the feature story survives the instrument, which is what makes it worth reading (it clears as readily as it flags). RULE: attribute a feature-study margin to the CONTRACT component in ABSOLUTE units, never the blended headline or a sub-noise %; the shared harness is pinned by cross-vertical guards (change it under the test-merge discipline). NCAAF-P1.4 is the next tripwire — same arm shape, inactive ONLY until a winner is promoted there.
- **🎓 A SUB-THRESHOLD MATCHED-PAIR DELTA IS MEASURED *NON-QUOTABLE*, NOT MERELY UNDECLARED — and two cv_power RENDER defaults would PUBLISH A MISLEADING re-test trigger for a gate that already PASSED (NCAAF-P2.1-S1b, 2026-08-17):** S1b gated (for the FIRST time) the +0.018 CRPS pace-composite-vs-8col-block matched delta that S1 had only REPORTED as a 'declared, never gated' attribution. It cleared DSR (0.969) but FAILED the multiple-testing correction (p=0.041 vs 0.025, the BINDING gate) and the overfitting-prob (0.331). ⭐ CLAIM-HYGIENE: that makes the +0.018 measured NON-QUOTABLE, not just undeclared — the representation choice is a TIE broken on MECHANISM (the 8-col block spans a lower-dim space via a ratio identity), NOT on evidence; a '0.3% better' line would not survive scrutiny. ⭐ READ THE TWO FAILS RIGHT: the multiple-testing shortfall is an EFFECT-SIZE gap, not sample-size (a 9th season is necessary-NOT-sufficient; a WIDER search RAISES the bar) ⇒ the honest lever is a LOWER-VARIANCE design (more rows/fold, a sharper metric), NEVER 're-run after more seasons'; the overfitting-prob fail on a 3-config field is a TIE signal (a middle finish already counts as an overfit event on 3 arms — structurally coarse), not fragility (the MH2/NF1.8 tied-field-high-PBO class). ⚠️ TWO cv_power INSTRUMENT DEFECTS (fixed at S1b's CALL-SITE, raw preserved; the SHARED fix is CARDED — Nth downstream hand-correction): (1) the null-classifier DEFAULTS TO GAUSSIAN MOMENT assumptions about shape while the binding statistic measures shape FROM THE DATA — they disagreed by 3 seasons here, and the Gaussian default would have PUBLISHED a 'come back with one more season' trigger for a gate that ALREADY PASSED (the MH2 misleading-trigger class; sibling of MH2.10's Normal-drawn-null bias for a variance statistic); (2) an all-arms-rejected field renders '+-8 folds (⇒ 0 total)' — a NEGATIVE data requirement = a mis-rendered state, not an instruction (the classify_null n_arms=1 fold-shortage mis-render, 5th instance). ⇒ until the shared fix lands, any vertical reading a cv_power re-test trigger must confirm the shape assumption is data-measured + reject a negative fold count.
- **🧪 A CALIBRATED NULL THAT PLACES A *VARIANCE* STATISTIC IN A NORMAL-DRAWN NULL IS BIASED TOWARD FINDING SCALE DEFECTS ON A SKEWED TARGET — use a SHAPE-MATCHED null, and it RE-FRAMES every earlier variance flag in the lineage (MH2.10, 2026-08-16; POWER_LIMITED on the morning tier):** the sampling variance of a variance statistic depends on the FOURTH moment (`Var(s²) = σ⁴·(2/(n−1) + κ/n)`), and MLB totals are right-skewed + leptokurtic against a symmetric Normal — so a Normal-DRAWN calibrated null (MH2.6's instrument) is systematically TOO NARROW for any variance statistic, and a SHAPE defect mechanically MANUFACTURES an apparent SCALE flag. CURE = a SHAPE-MATCHED null: resample the observed standardized residuals rescaled to variance EXACTLY 1, so the null hypothesis is precisely 'the σ scale is correct' with shape as a NUISANCE (⚠️ variance statistics ONLY — on a PIT statistic this would build the tested defect INTO the null). On MH2.10 it moved the headline flag p 0.052→0.100. ⭐ NOT LOCAL: ANY MH2-lineage calibrated-null study that put a variance statistic in a Normal-drawn null is biased toward finding defects on a skewed target — worth BACK-APPLYING; it re-frames how earlier variance flags across the program should be read. ⭐ SIBLING — **a σ-partition with a NEGATIVE rank correlation orders realized dispersion BACKWARDS, which is worse than under-powered and FORECLOSES a per-game σ fix (only a global multiplier survives):** MH2.10's `rms_var_z_sigma` flag (p=0.003) is INADMISSIBLE — the morning σ-partition fails the MH2.1 stratifier-validation lock with ρ=−0.042 (full) / −0.143 (recent). This is a DIFFERENT failure from MH2.6's post_lineup under-power (right sign, too little); a negative rank correlation is ANTI-informative → any future morning-σ work is limited to a GLOBAL SCALE MULTIPLIER until the partition validates (a measured roadmap constraint, not an oversight). ⭐ SIBLING (operating characteristics) — **if a PERFECTLY-CALIBRATED model returns the same verdict LABEL at this n, the label carries NO information — quote it as neither reassurance nor suspicion:** a flawless morning model at n=655 returns POWER_LIMITED 40/40 (the served label); the MDE at 80% power is σ×1.08 while the observed effect is σ×1.055, below the instrument's resolution. Measure `P(verdict | flawless model)` and state the honest 'not shown wrong, not shown right' rather than picking a side. The re-test trigger is stated in GAMES (≈2,200 served morning games — ~116 serving days, but crossing an off-season and RESETTING if the champion changes; the population is defined by the CHAMPION, not the calendar), never in calendar days.
- **🎲 A BOUNDARY-DEGENERATE LIKELIHOOD FITS A FALSE NULL THAT LOOKS COMPLETELY CLEAN — only a PRE-REGISTERED POSITIVE control catches it, and a NEGATIVE control must mirror the SHIP RULE not a bare 'closest' (MH2.8, 2026-08-16; INCUMBENT_STANDS on a real, served defect):** MH2.8 tested MH2.6's skew finding (the served total_runs Normal over-states P(over) ~5.6pts AT THE POSTED LINE) with an 8-arm pre-registered field. TWO build-time defects, each a reusable trap: **(1) ⭐ THE SKEW-NORMAL LIKELIHOOD IS MATHEMATICALLY FLAT AT ZERO-SKEW**, so the fitter reported 'no skew, converged successfully' on all 8 folds of OBVIOUSLY-skewed data → the skew arm became IDENTICAL to its own foil and the study reported 'nothing helps' with EVERY GATE GREEN — a false null indistinguishable from a real one. The pre-registered POSITIVE control (a known defect MUST be found) caught it; a negative control + green gates never could. A boundary-degenerate fit (a parameter whose likelihood is flat at its null value) silently collapses a candidate onto its foil → this is the strongest argument for the vacuity-floor discipline. **(2) ⭐ A NEGATIVE CONTROL MUST APPLY THE SHIP RULE'S THRESHOLD, not a bare 'who's closest':** MH2.8's negative control ranked by nearest arm (so on clean symmetric data it 'picked a skew arm ~⅓ of the time' = reads as 'the harness invents skew') while the SHIP rule requires a MEANINGFUL MARGIN — and 0 of 60 clean replicates produced a shippable margin (largest 9× below the bar vs a real-data margin ~17 SD out). ⛔ the clause stays FAILED (re-scoring against a bar chosen after seeing it fail is the E2.1-r inversion), but a successor's control must mirror the ship rule's margin. ⭐ SIBLING — **the DSR/significance gate refused a real, served, quality-clearing arm because ONE heterogeneous arm inflated its bar (the MH2.5/NF-W6b-C/S1 mechanism, Nth instance), AND a single 2.7% COVID season dominated the per-fold DSR:** the skew-normal recal cleared 9 of 10 ship clauses on BOTH windows incl. the served-row gate (PIT 0.042→0.031; posted-line P(over) gap +0.056→−0.025; the matched foil closes almost none → attributable to skew) but failed the deflated-Sharpe gate; meanwhile NGBoost Gamma is the ONE arm that PASSES the significance gate and FAILS the sharpness constraint (flat calibration bought at a materially worse predictive) — neither gate malfunctioning, the FIELD is heterogeneous. Dropping ONE 60-game COVID season (2.7% of data) moved the winner's per-fold SNR ~5× (0.46→2.30) and DSR 0.002→0.373 — the primary says 'nowhere near,' the sensitivity says 'close, the design refuses it' (run a leave-one-anomalous-season sensitivity). ⇒ the successor is a NARROWER coherent-family FRESH registration (diagnostic 0.79 vs the 0.95 bar — NOT a foregone pass), ⛔ never a re-cut of this field (MH2.2). And the honest PM tension: a displayed probability is provably ~5.6pts off with a fix that works on served data but didn't clear the pre-registered gate — HOLD (overriding a gate post-result is the exact failure this program has been burned by), but 'INCUMBENT_STANDS' ≠ 'nothing there.'
- **🧪 A §0.5 CALIBRATION STUDY MUST AUDIT THE *SERVED* ROWS (NOT A BACKTEST), SCORE AGAINST A CALIBRATED NULL (NOT JUST A BOOTSTRAP), AND PROVE ITS OWN HARNESS CAN FAIL — the vacuity-floor trap fires a whole INFERENCE HARNESS, not one clause (MH2.6, 2026-08-16, the served-calibration audit that correctly did NOT retrain on a 2-day blip):** four durable method lessons. **(1) ⭐ A BACKTEST CANNOT SEE A SERVING-PATH DEFECT** — every prior baseball calibration read (MH2.1, MH2.5) was over the training matrix; MH2.1's rollback happened precisely because a backtest conclusion didn't survive contact with the SERVED population. Audit the rows the app ACTUALLY SHOWED (de-duplicated to the served row, joined to realized finals, DuckDB-over-S3), not a backtest. **(2) ⭐ A BOOTSTRAP CI DESCRIBES A STATISTIC'S SPREAD; IT CANNOT ANSWER 'WOULD A PERFECTLY-CALIBRATED MODEL PRODUCE A WINDOW THIS ROUGH?'** — for that, score every statistic inside a CALIBRATED NULL (re-draw outcomes from the served predictive itself with n and per-game μ/σ/p̂ held fixed), and state a null as 'no defect larger than X' via an MDE curve — a window that CANNOT detect a gross defect (here 23 games couldn't see a 1.5-run level error or a 15-pt probability error at 80% power) carries NO information either way, which is the reusable answer to 'it feels rough.' **(3) ⭐⭐ THE VACUITY-FLOOR: a MC-null + multiplicity-correction harness has THREE compounding ways to return 'within noise' for EVERY input including a catastrophically broken model** — no multiplicity control across ~15 statistics (family-wise error ≈50% the OTHER way), a Monte-Carlo p-value that returns exactly 0 outside the draw range (survives any correction), and — the one to remember — BELOW A CERTAIN REP COUNT no statistic can ever clear the correction, so the smoke config was a machine that passes everything. Caught by the study's OWN pre-registered CLEAN-DATA negative control (9 of 20 perfectly-calibrated synthetic frames flagged before any real read); post-fix 0/40 clean false positives, detection 8/8 on gross defects. This is the NF1.7(a) 'a guard that cannot fail is worse than none' at the scale of a whole inference harness — and it applies to EVERY §0.5 study combining a MC null with a multiplicity correction (football + prospect included). **(4) ⭐ THE σ-STRATIFIER READING IS WINDOW-DEPENDENT (4th confirmation) — now QUANTIFIED:** the served-σ partition validates on the full era (2.05 SE, barely) and FAILS on 30 days (1.62 SE); the same partition passes on 634 games and fails on 375. σ's coefficient of variation is only 0.048, so it needs ~1,155 served games to clear the 2.0-SE bar → UNDER-POWERED, ⛔ NOT 'σ is fine.' A stratifier validation is population-specific (MH2.1), never a one-time certificate; when it fails, compute the games needed from the stratifier's CV rather than concluding either way. ⭐ THE FINDING THAT OUTLIVES THE STUDY (→ a pre-registered successor MH2.8, ⛔ NOT fired here): the served totals predictive is a SYMMETRIC Normal against a RIGHT-SKEWED target (realized z skew +0.735 — a blow-up inning has no left mirror; 57.3% of games below the predictive median), so at a line at the model's own mean it prints P(over)=0.500 while realized is 0.427 — P(over) over-stated ~7 points at the middle. This is the mechanism behind MH2.5's unexplained excess kurtosis.
