Phase 2 correctly did NOT fire — the defect is SHAPE (level unbiased, variance inside its null), and firing a scoped σ/level branch at an unscoped skew defect is 'fitting noise with extra steps.'
- **🏈🎯 A SYMMETRIC-NORMAL PREDICTIVE IS THE WRONG DEFAULT FOR *ANY* SCORING TOTAL — the same defect is now confirmed in TWO independent sports, and it is SKEW not tails (NCAAF-P2.5, 2026-08-19; corroborates MH2.6/MH2.8 above → PROGRAM-level, not per-vertical):** the NCAAF served total predictive is a symmetric Normal against a RIGHT-skewed target (fitted skew-normal α≈+2.12 on the total vs +1.02 on the margin; a shootout has no mirror below 0 points → the residual has a right tail and the median sits below the mean = a location bias). Modelling skew cut the total PIT deviation −62% (0.0170→0.0065) while IMPROVING CRPS; `skew_t`'s ν pinned at its Normal-limit and `student_t` alone tied → the mechanism is SKEW, confirmed NOT fat tails. ⇒ for any NEW scoring-total model (MLB/NCAAF/NFL/NCAAB) do NOT default to a symmetric predictive — carry an explicit skew/asymmetric form from day one. FOUR reusable §0.5 sub-lessons from the same study: **(a) RE-VERIFY A STORY'S MOTIVATING FIGURE AGAINST THE *SERVED* CONFIG, NEVER THE CARD** — P2.5's card cited total PITdev 0.0218 from the SUPERSEDED `strength_only` contract; the SERVED `strength_pace` was 0.0173 PIT-flat with the real failure a `pit_mean_dev` 0.0263 a prior story had already largely repaired, so measuring vs the card would have handed every arm a 0.0045 UNEARNED head start + set the wrong bar (the stale-premise class at the METRIC-BASELINE level → pre-flight every card's premise against the running system before trusting it). **(b) `½E|X−X'|` FLOORS A *PIT* METRIC BUT NOT *CRPS*** — an over-dispersed arm 'beats' that oracle, so a CRPS-judged bake-off needs a PEEKING oracle at matched family+sample (the NF-W6d per-form-ceiling lesson), and a per-form ceiling refit by each arm's MLE while judging on CRPS DIVERGES for a non-Gaussian scale transform (a standing instrument gap). **(c) A DISTRIBUTIONAL-FORM SIGN ERROR CAN LEAVE EVERY HEADLINE METRIC PLAUSIBLE** — a lower-tail sign error kept CRPS/PIT/coverage/leaderboard all plausible across TWO full runs; only a SHAPE-INVARIANT guard (the tail is monotone + lands outside the outer knot) caught it → guard the shape invariant, not just the scores. **(d) `DSR_UNREACHABLE` MEANS A LOWER-VARIANCE DESIGN, NOT MORE DATA** — the best-CRPS `key_number` arm (+0.0287, 7/8 folds, PBO 0.000) failed DSR purely because its per-fold Sharpe 1.243 < the field's SR0 1.497, and √(n−1) scales a positive gap but cannot CREATE one → no seasons clear it; the fix is a SHARPER MATCHED STATISTIC (paired per-game deltas + a blocked bootstrap), a design change (NF-D18/MH2 class). And a clean conditional-variance NULL attributed correctly: the driver set loses 0/8 AND its matched PERMUTATION anchor BEATS it → the drivers are absent, not weak.
- **📐 A GRID-MEAN / TRUNCATED-SUPPORT RANKING POINT READS A BIASED POINT OFF A PERFECTLY-CALIBRATED DISTRIBUTION — a cross-model/cross-generator LEVEL gap can be an artifact of HOW THE POINT IS READ, not a calibration defect (NF-W8-0, 2026-08-19):** the NF-W8 cross-position layer found a real QB level gap (−0.470 PPR vs realized; QB|WR −0.359, QB|TE −0.319 survive BH) while EVERY position's PIT is flat — because the consumer-visible ranking point (a 199-level grid MEAN) TRUNCATES the outer 0.5% tails and QB's right tail is the heaviest, so the point reads 0.371 PPR below the direct-points construction. ⇒ when comparing POINTS across models/generators, the summary statistic (a truncated/grid mean) is a SHARED ASSUMPTION to verify — complete the tail (a DETERMINISTIC transform) before concluding a level gap is real; the prime successor NF-W8-0b is exactly that (tail-complete the point with the NF-MARGIN exponential-tail machinery, NO fitting, NO contact with the certified distributions). THREE reusable siblings: **(a) ⭐ A UNIFORM PER-GROUP LEVEL SHIFT CANCELS IN VOR/RANK-SPACE** — a group's own replacement level absorbs it, so a cross-group level artifact corrupts RAW-POINT surfaces (start/sit, lineup totals) but is STRUCTURALLY IMMUNE on a VOR-ranked board (measured 0.000 rank moves) as long as the group isn't cross-pooled (QB isn't FLEX; a SUPERFLEX league loses the shield) → scope a level gap's blast radius to the SURFACE, don't block the immune one. **(b) A TRAILING-HISTORY-FITTED PER-GROUP CONSTANT CHASES A MOVING TARGET** — the realized cross-position level DRIFT (0.511 PPR, a climatology anchor) was ≈ the artifact itself (0.489), so a level constant fit on prior history is measured-OUT; prefer a deterministic transform over a fitted constant when the target is non-stationary (NF1.8's two-sided climatology anchor is what proved the drift). **(c) A SWAP/ACTIVITY CLAUSE NEEDS A MATERIALITY FLOOR** — NF-W8-0's swap clause refused on 0.037/0.095-PPR shifts (statistically active, tiny SEs) an order of magnitude below the family's own detection floor while the position actually indicted (QB) collapsed decisively (NF-W6 'demonstrable≠material', landing on the clause itself). ⚠️ Harness: `round(…, 6)` on stored scores CAPS a 1e-9 reproduction pin → the decisive run returns UNDEFINED while reproducing perfectly (a smoke-caught bug). And the QB Option-B governance lesson: `dsr_ok` is a SHIPPING gate, NOT a CONSUMPTION bar for a projection product — a projection consumer may consume the best-calibrated/best-scoring construction under a FORWARD-registered, caveated, second-reader-flagged decision WITHOUT relaxing the ship bar (⛔ that is a consumption decision, never a re-certification — E2.1-r).
- **🔑 AN OAUTH GRANT THAT SUCCEEDS ON OIDC BUT 401s ON THE ACTUAL RESOURCE = THE ENTITLEMENT ISN'T ATTACHED TO THE APP, NOT A TOKEN/SCOPE BUG — and permission BINDS AT CONSENT TIME (NF-C0-Yahoo-SPIKE, 2026-08-19):** the same valid Yahoo token returned 200 on `openid/v1/userinfo` and 401 `oauth_problem='additional_authorization_required'` on every Fantasy resource — because Fantasy access is a property of the APPROVED APP (Yahoo sends NO Fantasy scope param, so the gap is invisible until a real resource call is made), and signing the data agreement did NOT attach it. ⇒ (a) diagnose a resource-level 401-with-OIDC-200 as a PROVISIONING gap (chase the vendor), not a client bug — the OIDC 200 is the control that eliminates bad-token/bad-URL/wrong-account/empty-list; (b) enabling a late-attached entitlement needs a FRESH consent (the existing grant won't silently acquire it); (c) an `is_enabled()` availability GATE that refuses the flow until the entitlement is confirmed is load-bearing — here its docstring predicted the exact failure, and it surfaced only because the probe bypassed the gate → keep the gate closed until a real resource call succeeds. SIBLING (box/laptop diagnosis): STATIC AWS keys in the shell BEAT `AWS_PROFILE`, and a `baseball-access-user` key that can't read the app's SSM params surfaces (by a swallowed-error design) as 'not configured yet' — a PROVISIONING verdict masking a PERMISSIONS one; prefix `env -u AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID -u AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY -u AWS_SESSION_TOKEN AWS_PROFILE=…` when a config-read 'fails' inexplicably. And `?yahoo=connected` / a callback that reports 'no code in that URL' on the run that PROVES the handshake (the deployed callback already spent the single-use code) — the scariest-looking output is the success path.
- **🏈 VOR IS A STARTER-SCARCITY CURRENCY — 'best value on the board' LATE IN A DRAFT IS STRUCTURALLY A BACKUP TE/QB, and a bench seat is not a starter slot (NF-C-LDA-1 #948, 2026-08-19):** replacement level sits at the STARTER cutoff, so once a 12-team room consumes the ~35 WR / ~25 RB who clear it (by round 8, since every team starts 2-3), positive VOR survives only at the one-per-team positions (12 TE / 12 QB) where a bench player helps you LEAST → 'take the best VOR' hands out a backup TE/QB every late round. The fix is a bench-specific score: P(you actually need him) × his marginal points over the next man up (an 'insurance' rule; measured +77.3 pts/season vs the naive rule, 118/120 paired drafts, 83% of a peeking-oracle ceiling; a plausible-looking 'scale VOR by startable seats' foil LOST to doing nothing, −26.6). ⇒ for any roster/lineup optimizer, score a BENCH candidate on insurance value, never on the starter-scarcity currency. TWO reusable siblings: **(a) A PARITY HARNESS PROVES TWO ENGINES AGREE, NOT THAT EITHER IS RIGHT** — NF-C-LDA-1's Python/TS parity gate held the two byte-identical THROUGH both shipped roster-counting bugs (an IR spot counted as a draftable pick; a filled FLEX seat counted toward TE capacity) → agreement and correctness are different claims; a parity gate needs a correctness assertion beside it (the E9.61 two-renderers family, on the agreement side). **(b) A RULE/CONSTRAINT WITH ≥2 IMPLEMENTATIONS NEEDS A PINNED EXHAUSTIVENESS REGISTRY** — the K/DST reserve constraint had 3 implementations (Python engine, TS engine, mock CPU), all defective, and the registry's first sweep found a 4th and 5th (the auction reserve) — the 'one logical rule, many owners' class (INC-30/36/38), on the app engine. ⭐ And the process rule the session earned: EVERY defect was found by DRAFTING, invisible to a green suite, because the broken and healthy states looked identical until something forced them apart (a `2>/dev/null` that made 'no such file' == 'empty file'; an inactive oracle scoring identically to an honest arm) → one deliberate LIVE pass per draft-season feature is a GATE, not a nicety.
- **📉 A 'POINT READ OFF A QUANTILE GRID' CARRIES TWO OPPOSITE BIASES, AND A DETERMINISTIC PER-GROUP CORRECTION'S CEILING IS THE *SPREAD* OF ITS PER-GROUP DELTAS — AN IDENTITY, NOT AN ESTIMATE (NF-W8-0b, 2026-08-19; closes the NF-W8-0 tail hypothesis):** the 199-level grid mean both DROPS the outer tails (worst for a heavy tail) AND over-weights the covered mass by 200/199 = 0.5% of the position's LEVEL — and for QB (heaviest tail AND highest level) the two nearly CANCEL, so the tail-completion moved every position ~in parallel (Δ 0.033–0.052 PPR) and the cross-position gap barely closed (QB|WR −0.372→−0.362). ⇒ a pairwise gap moves only by the DIFFERENCE of two positions' deltas, so a deterministic per-group transform is bounded by the SPREAD of those deltas (0.0193 PPR vs a 0.36 artifact = 19× short) — compute that spread BEFORE running: if the correction moves all groups in parallel it CANNOT close a cross-group gap at any magnitude. The NF-W8-0 §12.3d 'the gap is the truncated tail' hypothesis is REFUTED with a number — the tail channel is 4.9% of QB's gap; ~95% lives in the distribution BODY, so the successor is a BODY-level comparison (assembled QB vs direct_points), NOT another tail story. THREE reusable siblings: **(a) DERIVE A TRANSFORM'S β FROM THE BANK'S OWN QUANTILE SPACING, NOT FROM REALIZED OUTCOMES** — an outcome-fitted constant re-imports the non-stationarity (0.511 drift > the 0.489 artifact) AND under-extends at the indicted position (NF-MARGIN2); and the `0.995` re-weight is LOAD-BEARING (without it a degenerate bank returns 1.005·c, a multiplicative level bias that manufactures the very cross-position differential being measured). **(b) SCOPE A RETEST TRIGGER TO THE FAMILY IT APPLIES TO** — family B's fitted contest went POWER_LIMITED (+2-fold trigger), but family A's deterministic null is ARITHMETICALLY bounded (no fold count widens a fixed spread) → reading B's fold trigger onto A is the NF-D18 misleading-trigger class; the record must name which family the trigger scopes to. **(c) a MATERIALITY FLOOR from the design's OWN pairwise MDEs (median) reclassifies precise-but-immaterial shifts inactive** — a design quantity registered forward (NF-W6 demonstrable≠material, shipped as the swap-clause floor: QB stays active + passes 0.358→0.076, WR/TE go correctly inactive). Process: a long run straddling a merge ORPHANED the record commits (PR merged at pre-run head; `gh pr checks` green on a 2-pushes-old SHA; `head.sha` frozen while the branch ref advanced) — re-check PR state before pushing a long-run result (E9.60/E8.6).
- **🗄️ A NESTED NUMERIC MAP READ FROM DynamoDB NEEDS A DEEP CONVERTER, AND A TEST THAT NEVER CROSSES THE STORE BOUNDARY CANNOT CATCH IT (NF-C7 #962, 2026-08-20 — an operator-found prod bug):** a shallow `_from_dynamo` returns a nested count map's values as `Decimal` one level down, and a validator's `isinstance(v,(int,float))` rejects every one → account defaults silently reverted to zero after a logout/token-refresh. 18 tests missed it because each handed a DICT to a Pydantic model, which coerces top-level `Decimal→int` — they tested the MODEL, never the STORE. ⇒ a nested numeric map from DynamoDB needs a deep converter, and a store round-trip test (not a dict→model test) is the only thing that catches it (`portfolio` escapes only because its numbers happen to sit one level up). FOUR reusable siblings from the same story, ALL found by drafting / a live pass / a measurement, NONE by a green suite: **(a) `pts/games` (a per-game RATE) divides by a tiny denominator for exactly the low-usage players a bench comparison is about** — Easton Stick 40.3/game → recommended round 10 against a −189 VOR; guard the denominator on any per-game rate. **(b) A CI SPEED-UP CLAIM ON A JOB WITH A LARGE RUN-TO-RUN SPREAD (285s here) NEEDS n≥5 MEDIANS, NOT A BEFORE/AFTER PAIR** — two retractions on the same job (36%→19%→the real effect −0.5s); the measure-before-crediting family (E11.24) on CI timing, and the real win was elsewhere (the slow gate was 11s of per-worker COLLECTION, not Monte-Carlo → hand pytest the marked files, keep the marker as the selector). **(c) A STUDY ARM CLAIMING TO MEASURE THE SHIPPED ENGINE THAT RE-DERIVES THE RANKING WITH ITS OWN `max(recs, key=score)` — WHERE `score` ISN'T THE ENGINE'S SORT KEY — MEASURES SOMETHING ELSE AND REPORTS IT AS SHIPPED** (drive the REAL engine — the NF-C0e wired≠invoked / E9.61 two-renderers family; the tell was a re-run coming back byte-identical after a change that provably moved the engine). **(d) A USER 'TARGET/QUOTA' THAT IS FLOOR-ONLY DOES NOTHING ON A POSITION THE USER ALREADY HOLDS** — a target must be a CAP as well as a floor (short/neutral/satisfied, ordering-only so the displayed score is never corrupted; the reserve constraint still outranks it by construction).
- **📐 DECOMPOSE FOLD VARIANCE BEFORE NAMING THE DSR LEVER — a fitted coefficient CLIMBING across folds looks like non-stationarity/DRIFT but is usually SAMPLING NOISE, and the lever is ROWS PER FOLD not seasons (NF-W8-0c, 2026-08-20):** NF-W8-0c's per-fold δ climbed 0.40→0.93 and the first read was 'the target is non-stationary, track the drift' — the decomposition REFUTED it: fold-to-fold SD 0.261 vs within-fold SE 0.233 ⇒ ~80% of the fold-scale variance is sampling noise, genuine between-fold ≈0.117, sign stable 8/8 negative. ⇒ the three axes of a DSR refusal are FIELD (MH2.5), RETURN-SERIES (NCAAF-P2.1), and TARGET VARIANCE = rows/fold (this) — measure which BEFORE naming a successor; a drift-tracking story would have spent itself on a lever that is 80% noise. THREE reusable siblings: **(a) FOUR dsr_ok-ALONE REFUSALS ON ONE POSITION IS A STATEMENT ABOUT THE EVAL DESIGN, NOT THE POSITION** — QB was refused by dsr_ok alone in NF-W7f/W7h/W7j/W8-0c while passing every other clause (in W8-0c the winner CLOSED the cross-position gap, PIT 7/7 better than incumbent, PBO 0.0) → run a cheap design/power instrument (the rows-per-fold × fold-count frontier: can an effect of this SIZE clear the bar at ANY design point?) BEFORE funding another modeling story into the same wall; if no point clears, the fix is a registered-FORWARD gate-design change, ⛔ never a post-hoc relaxation (E2.1-r). **(b) A FALL-THROUGH VERDICT STRING CAN BE WRONG-IN-FACT** — NF-W8-0c's registered 'the gap survives every arm' was FALSE (the winner closed it); leave the STATE as registered (E2.1-r) but correct the wrong-in-fact prose in the first paragraph a reader meets — don't silently re-label the state. **(c) TWO MECHANISMS ON THE SAME CELL (a calibration recal + a level shift), plausibly OPPOSITE directions, MUST be registered JOINTLY and the 2×2 measured** — NF-W7f raises QB|passing_yards' zero atom (lowers the mean) while NF-W8-0c needs its level UP; fixing them sequentially is the NF-W7e non-additivity trap (burn two stories, land back here). Housekeeping: a long run + a standing PR orphans the record when the PR merges at a commit behind the tip (caught NF-W8-0b AND 0c — E9.60).
- **🕰️ A 'FILL-NULLs-ONLY' INTRADAY SWEEP FREEZES A 'CLOSING'/FINAL VALUE HOURS EARLY — and a DRY-RUN THAT RETURNS BEFORE THE WRITE LOOP IS NOT A REHEARSAL (E11.24 P1, 2026-08-20):** predict_today's intraday `_backfill_outcomes()` sweeps ran MID-SLATE and only filled NULL `closing_market_prob`, so a game's 'closing' price froze at an ~18:00-21:00 UTC snapshot and stuck (game 822859: stored 0.606 vs the true last pre-game 0.592) — a value NAMED 'closing' captured hours before the close, systematically, since the sweep shipped. ⇒ any enrichment writing a 'final'/'closing'/'settled' value must be BOUNDED to rows whose event is actually OVER (`dt < current_game_date()`), never run mid-window on a fill-if-NULL basis. TWO reusable siblings: **(a) A DRY-RUN THAT SKIPS SERIALISATION IS NOT A REHEARSAL** — `--dry-run` returned before the write loop, so it validated the parity ARITHMETIC and nothing about whether rows could be WRITTEN; the real run then died on partition 1 (a `loaded_at` coercion). A dry-run must build every partition's actual write payload. **(b) A KEY-UNIQUENESS CHECK MUST BE `group by … having count(*)>1` AGAINST THE SHIPPED ARTIFACT, not a dict comparison** — `--verify` built a dict, which COLLAPSED the duplicates silently and reported 10,501 while the view returned 10,513; a dict-based dedup check cannot see the dups it dedups (the vacuous-guard family).
- **🏈 THE VOR 'SHIELD' IS ADDITIVE-ONLY — a MULTIPLICATIVE per-position correction RE-OPENS cross-position placement and MUST get the whole-board read; and the build-time face-validity check ranks by PPR while the SERVED boards rank by VOR/config, so a placement breach is INVISIBLE at publish (NF-TR2b, 2026-08-20):** NF-W8-0 proved a per-group level shift cancels in VOR space (a group's own replacement absorbs it, 0.000 rank moves) — TRUE only for an ADDITIVE shift. TR2b is MULTIPLICATIVE (`vor → k·vor` does NOT cancel + re-runs the greedy FLEX allocation, a cross-position draft on points), so citing the shield to SKIP the placement read would have skipped exactly the needed check. ⇒ any MULTIPLICATIVE per-position correction (a k re-fit, a rookie recal, an NF-W8 unification) MUST run the whole-board cross-position placement read (`run_nf_tr2b_placement_read` reads the published artifact, ~10s); ⚠️ and the shield does NOT hold under SUPERFLEX (QB is cross-pooled — and we publish 2 superflex configs). ⭐ THE INVISIBLE-BREACH: the NF-D18/D20 rookie placement cap runs ONLY in selection harnesses, never against the PUBLISHED board; the build-time `rookie_board_face_validity` check ranks by PPR POINTS while the 14 served boards rank by VOR PER CONFIG → a config-dependent placement breach is structurally invisible (a `standard_12` rookie-cap breach sat in prod unnoticed until this read — the E11.30 'detection exists but never runs where it matters' class) → wire the placement read as a PUBLISH-TIME gate. TWO siblings: **(a) A UNILATERAL VETERAN LEVEL CORRECTION MAKES THE UNCORRECTED (ROOKIE) LEG THE ASYMMETRIC ONE** — correcting one tier re-prices it against an uncorrected sibling; the cap guards rookies placed too HIGH but NOTHING guards too LOW → measure the uncorrected leg's level before treating a one-tier fix as complete. **(b) TWO OPPOSITE-SIGN LEVEL FINDINGS ON DIFFERENT SURFACES MUST BE RECONCILED WHERE THEY MEET, NOT BEFORE** — TR2b says QB veterans run ~7% HOT on the SEASON board (k=0.929) while NF-W8-0 reads QB −0.47 LOW on the WEEKLY assembled layer; different surfaces/units/windows, NOT a claimed conflict — but NF-W8 unification (all 4 positions on one generator + one level recal) is where they meet and must be checked coherent. Process: pre-flight 'deploy-held'/'code-ready' cards against the RUNNING system before assignment (this card was 5 days stale — TR2b was already live); a 'rerun interval revalidation' task is a MAIN-CHECKOUT job (`sports.duckdb` + panel caches gitignored, NF-INFRA1, absent in a worktree).
- **📐 A SHARED VARIANCE LEVER (rows/fold, folds, draws, a proportionally sharper estimator) IS DETERMINISTICALLY VOID FOR dsr_ok — 'get a lower-variance design' is the MISLEADING remedy that sent four QB records at the same wall (NF-W8-0d, 2026-08-21; the lockstep invariant, generalising NF-W7k + MH2):** `deflated_sharpe` reads the winner's Sharpe SR AND the deflation benchmark `SR0 = std(trial Sharpes)·z(N)` — and THE WINNER IS ONE OF THOSE TRIALS. A design change that scales every arm's per-fold dispersion by a common `c` scales every trial Sharpe by `1/c`, hence SR0 by `1/c`, hence `SR − SR0 ↦ (SR − SR0)/c`: **its SIGN is invariant.** Clearing needs `SR > SR0`; a shared-variance lever cannot create that — not merely ineffective but COUNTER-PRODUCTIVE (measured DSR 0.165 → 0.042 → 0.015 as dispersion ×0.25 → ×0.01). ⇒ when `DSR_UNREACHABLE` fires do NOT prescribe 'more rows/seasons/draws/a sharper metric' and do NOT publish a season/fold/row re-test trigger (the invariant is deterministic → 'come back with more data' is the actively-misleading NF-D18 direction); the only real levers are a DIFFERENTIAL-variance design (shrink the winner's dispersion NOT the field's — untested, not refuted), a BIGGER effect, or a genuine absence. `fp_dsr_frontier.py` answers 'would a lower-variance design clear this gate?' for any §0.5 leg with a published per-fold record (the NF-W7k draw-ceiling shape, one axis over). TWO sub-findings: **(a) AN INACTIVE ARM DOMINATES V AND IS SCALE-FREE** — 72.9% of the deflation dispersion came from one arm already measured inactive (ceiling −0.0018 PPR); shrinking noise shrinks its mean AND sd together, so SR0/SR stays flat across the frontier → DSR-CONV should exclude a MEASURED-INACTIVE arm from V (keyed on the per-form oracle ceiling, NOT the leaderboard; a V-composition rule, not a field trim — the arm keeps paying full multiplicity). **(b) A CLASSIFIER'S REMEDY TEXT CORRECTED N TIMES DOWNSTREAM IS A DEFECT IN THE INSTRUMENT** — `cv_power.classify_null`'s `DSR_UNREACHABLE` trigger literally names 'a lower-variance design' and sent NF-W7j/W7k/W8-0c at this wall; the fix is to make it COMPUTE the lockstep check (MH2.7 lesson-(i); a shared instrument → re-anchor its cross-vertical guards, don't weaken them).
- **⚛️ THREE FRONTEND BUGS THAT ALL RENDER/PASS BUT SILENTLY MISFIRE (NF-DS, 2026-08-21):** (a) ⭐ **A RADIX `<Dialog onOpenChange>` FIRES ONLY FOR DISMISSALS RADIX ITSELF INITIATES** (Escape / overlay click / close button) — NEVER for an `open` prop flipped from OUTSIDE (a parent effect, a button that sets state). So any side effect wired through `onOpenChange` — here a 'modal shown' analytics event — silently NEVER fires on the commonest open paths (an auto-open effect, a manual trigger). ⇒ key a modal-open side effect on a `useEffect([open])`, NOT `onOpenChange` (which is dismissal-only). (b) **A SUBSTRING CLAIM/OVERCLAIM DENYLIST IS NEGATION-BLIND** — `claim-denylist.ts` substring-matches 'guaranteed', so an HONEST negated sentence ('not a guaranteed finish') trips it exactly as hard as a real guarantee → the honest copy is what the guard blocks (the NF-C6P3 negation-blind-scan family). Reword to avoid the token, or make the scan negation-aware. (c) **A PLAYWRIGHT `page.waitForResponse()` RACES AN ALREADY-FIRED REQUEST** — a listener armed AFTER an auto-open effect already triggered the fetch times out intermittently under parallel-worker load (the request already completed). ⇒ read the DOM's actual `src` and `page.request.get()` it, don't race a live network listener. ⭐ And the reusable no-backend pattern worth copying: a share artifact (OG image + landing page) built as a PURE FUNCTION of its URL query string (encode/decode round-trips, malformed→null never throws, a placeholder fallback so a mangled link never serves a 500) needs zero server persistence + zero gateway/authorizer surface (NF3.2 not-applicable, confirmed not skipped) — but the OG/share URL must hardcode the PRODUCTION origin (a shared link is permanent, not a preview alias), so it self-activates only on the prod deploy.
- **📊 A COVERAGE FLOOR AT NOMINAL IS A ~50% COIN-FLIP FALSE-REJECT REGARDLESS OF n — derive the floor from n + a pre-registered false-reject TARGET (exact one-sided Binomial), NEVER a flat nominal point-floor, and NEVER from the observed value (NF-D22, 2026-08-21):** a flat 0.80 point-floor rejects a PERFECTLY-calibrated 80% band `P(Binom(n,0.80)/n < 0.80)` ≈ 0.39-0.50 at EVERY n (0.500 at n=148) — and it does NOT improve with n (more rows buy power to detect a SMALLER true shortfall, never a lower false-alarm rate), so 'wait for more seasons' was never a fix. At the FAMILY level (every per-group floor must hold) the whole re-validation falsely fired ~93% of the time on a healthy product. CURE = `power_floor()`: the largest required covered-count `k` with `P(Binom(n,nominal) < k) ≤ target`, floor = k/n — inputs are the group's n + a PRE-REGISTERED target (NF1.8's Tier-2 0.05, written down long before), and NOTHING else (it takes no coverage argument → structurally can't be reverse-engineered from the failing value = E2.1-r-proof). It self-attenuates `√(1/n)` → converges to nominal, so NO 'thin-group' list is needed (a boundary would be a chosen degree of freedom drawn around the group that failed). ⚠️ TWO-SIDED validation is load-bearing: a floor of 0.0 satisfies 'a correct band passes' VACUOUSLY → also assert 'a materially-short band still FAILS' (a 0.65 band caught 99% at n=148), and gate BOTH. Family false-reject fell 0.93→0.15 while a broken band is still caught. ⚠️ a PASS is 'not shown broken at this n', never 'shown right' — publish the floor's resolution (n=148 detects a rookie-RB defect only down to ~0.708), and REPORT multiplicity rather than Bonferroni-correcting (that would LOOSEN every floor — the one adjustment to distrust from a story whose effect is a previously-refused publish clearing). ⭐ Use the EXACT bound: a normal approximation misses its own advertised rate by up to +0.011 at small n. Reusable for any per-group coverage/calibration floor (MLB, NCAAF, prospect). And the governance shape: fixing a gate defect that had refused a ratified improvement is clean ONLY if the fix is derived + validated BEFORE the refused consequence is re-scored (NF-D22 did; the refused story stays CLOSED until the PM records a NEW disposition against the fixed gate, then flips — never a bare flag flip, which `assert_coherent` refuses at import).
- **🎲 A RANDOM-SIDE PLACEBO IS A WEAK DEGENERATE IN A vs-CLOSE / HIT-RATE EVAL — a spurious edge rarely looks like RANDOMNESS, it looks like quietly AGREEING WITH A DIRECTIONAL BIAS; the discriminating anchors are pre-registered SIDE-BIAS degenerates, not a coin flip (NCAAF-VAL1, 2026-08-20):** VAL1's random-side placebo passed all six cells and discriminated NOTHING; the work was done by the pre-registered directional anchors `always_home` (0.5121) and `always_under` (0.5362), which BOTH beat the model in wk1-3 and fired the 'beats every degenerate' clause. ⇒ carry BOTH a random placebo AND directional side-bias anchors (`always_home`/`always_over`/`always_under`) into every vs-close leg, any sport — a model that has quietly aligned with a directional market tilt beats a coin flip but must not beat the directional degenerate. ⭐ SIBLING (the discovery mechanism, reusable): an AGGREGATE PIT / calibration check is STRUCTURALLY BLIND to a small consistent μ-vs-close OFFSET — VAL1's served total sits above the close in 58.5% of games (a large side-split) while realized PIT stays flat (0.0170), because a ~1pt offset against σ≈16.6 can't dent aggregate flatness but is exactly what makes a 58/42 split. A directional tilt versus the MARKET is only visible in a MARKET-RELATIVE read (our μ vs the close), never in a realized-outcome calibration gate — run both when a model prices a total/spread. And the founding-premise lesson: the most-cited 'where the edge lives' subset (early-season college softness) came back the WORST bucket, non-monotone — test a motivating premise with a pre-registered stratification BEFORE scoping epics around it (VAL1 removed the hypothesis; it does not refute the edge program, but it lowers the prior).
- **🧩 A SUPER-ADDITIVE INTERACTION MEANS THE HALVES ARE RESCUING EACH OTHER — refit EVERY parameter INSIDE each cell of the joint grid, never once across it, or the marginal reading of either half is actively misleading (NF-W8-0e, 2026-08-21; NF-W7e reproduced with an INDEPENDENT mechanism + a MEASURED cause):** two corrections touching the same quantity (QB|passing_yards {zero-mass off/on}×{conditional level none/shift/scale}) registered JOINTLY as a 2×N grid measured Δ_Z −0.155, Δ_C-alone −13.24, Δ_joint −0.975, interaction +12.42 (joint÷sum-of-halves 0.073). The conditional correction ALONE costs 13.6× what it costs ALONGSIDE the zero-mass fix — because with the atom left wrong ~21 pts of probability mass that belongs at zero sits in the conditional region, drags the fitted mean ~35 yds low and inflates the fitted shift 2.4×, which is then applied to that mass too. ⇒ a SEQUENTIAL story would have measured Δ_C=−13.24 first and abandoned a correction that is directionally RIGHT — the wrong conclusion off a perfectly correct number. RULE: when ≥2 mechanisms touch ONE quantity, register them JOINTLY, read the INTERACTION, and refit every parameter WITHIN each grid cell; never fix one then the other (NF-W7e), and never trust a marginal Δ when the interaction is large. ⭐ SIBLING (the honest close this produced): the QB cross-position level gap is now refused at EVERY layer for MATERIALLY DIFFERENT reasons — assembly-anchor (NF-W8-0) / tail-bounded, 95% is body (0b) / dsr_ok-INSTRUMENTAL, the arm works but the gate's design can't certify it (0c) / design-frontier EMPTY (0d, the lockstep invariant) / cell-metric SUBSTANTIVE, the arm closes the gap but loses its own registered CRPS decisively (0e). A per-layer refusal TABLE is the honest artifact; 0e's is a genuine trade not a gate artifact (the cell says don't correct, the assembled level says correct, assembled CRPS is indifferent = the pricing-optimal≠discrimination-optimal family). ⛔ re-reading the field on a DIFFERENT primary after CRPS refused is the E2.1-r inversion. Consumer note: the flagship VOR board is STRUCTURALLY SHIELDED (a uniform per-position level shift cancels in VOR — own replacement absorbs it, QB isn't FLEX-eligible; measured 0.000 rank moves) — only raw-point cross-position surfaces (start/sit, lineup totals) and superflex are gated by a QB level gap.
- **⚖️ DEFLATION GATES BIND CLAIMS; MEASUREMENT GATES BIND CONSUMPTION — a `best_alpha=0` projection CONSUMER may gate on PIT+CRPS even when `dsr_ok` fails, but ONLY forward-registered, caveated-by-a-mechanical-guard, reversible, and with the model-layer fixes measured-exhausted (NF-W8 QB Option-B, APPROVED-WITH-CONDITIONS by an independent second reader 2026-08-21):** DSR/deflation deflates a SEARCH — it asks whether a winner's margin over its own field survives selection bias, the right question before STAKING A CLAIM (an edge, a certification). A projection CONSUMER makes a DIFFERENT claim ("this input is calibrated AND better than the alternative"), answered directly by PIT (a per-fold MEASUREMENT, not a selection statistic — here 0.0281 at 8/8 folds vs the incumbent's 0/8) and CRPS-vs-matched-foil (p=0.0121, PBO 0.0). When the alternative is an incumbent that FAILS calibration on every fold and the within-family choice is low-stakes (near-tied arms ⇒ cost ≤ the family spread), there is NO analog of the catastrophic error DSR exists to prevent ⇒ holding consumption on `dsr_ok` INDEFINITELY is "a decision wearing a gate's clothing" — especially once NF-W8-0d proved NO feasible design clears it (a gate no true effect of the observed magnitude can pass at ANY achievable design is not discriminating at this scale; the lockstep invariant). **RULES: (1)** a consumer may gate on the MEASUREMENT (PIT/CRPS), not the DEFLATION — but ONLY under a FORWARD registration (adopting it AFTER a failed gate is E2.1-r laundering), with the caveat riding every downstream row via a MECHANICAL exhaustiveness guard (not prose — the NF-C0e wired≠invoked cure), and fully reversible/deploy-held; **(2)** PARK is the WRONG disposition once every remedy lever is measured-exhausted — park implies waiting for information that no longer exists, recreating the "refusing forever on an exhausted bar" state; **(3)** the STANDING to make this call is EARNED by a governance regime that demonstrably lets its gates say NO (NF-D21 was refused and STAYED refused; the 0c/0d/0e refusals all stood under the same discipline) — it is a scoped judgment about WHICH GATE BINDS WHICH CONSUMER, NEVER a threshold move, and a `dsr_ok` shipping/certification bar is untouched for everyone else.
- **🔢 `df[mask].reset_index(drop=True)` THEN INDEXING A PARALLEL ARRAY WITH THE RESET INDEX READS THE WRONG ROWS — the symptom is NOISE, indistinguishable from an honest null (NCAAF-VAL2, 2026-08-21):** `reset_index(drop=True)` renumbers the surviving rows 0..n−1, so using that reset index (or `df.index` of the reset frame) to index a SEPARATE parallel array keyed by ORIGINAL row position (e.g. an `(n_games, n_draws)` predictive-draw matrix) reads the FIRST n rows of that array — a DIFFERENT row's data, NOT a recovery of the original positions. NCAAF's vs-close CLV eval did exactly this across P1.4 / S1-serve / VAL1: **100% of 4,182 rows misindexed** (model-side-agrees-with-sign(μ−close) **0.697 as-coded vs 0.980 repaired**, where 0.980 is the arithmetic prediction, not a chosen tolerance). It survived for months because the SHAPES are right, NO exception is raised, CI mocks all IO, and a misaligned vs-close hit rate reads **~0.51 — EXACTLY what a correct read AND a completely broken read both produce** (⇒ a broken eval is GUARANTEED a null; it cannot EARN one — "a misaligned evaluation survives as a false null"). **RULE: to recover original row positions after a boolean mask, take `np.flatnonzero(mask)` BEFORE any reset (or keep the UN-reset `.index`); NEVER index a parallel positional array with a post-`reset_index(drop=True)` index. Guard it with an AST/tripwire that fails when a masked-then-reset index feeds a parallel-array `[...]`, and carry the required re-run list in the tripwire's docstring so the fix cannot ship without the downstream re-runs.** Not sport-specific — any masked-then-reset join to a draw/score/prediction matrix. And the meta-lesson: **when a study's own headline metric can be produced identically by a correct pipeline AND a totally broken one (a ~0.50 hit rate, a ~0 effect), the metric cannot by itself certify the pipeline — a second, mechanism-level check (here: does the model's side agree with `sign(μ−close)` at the rate the distributional form arithmetically requires?) is what tells the two apart.**
- **🧬 A "POINT IS PRIMITIVE, STAT LINE BACK-DERIVED BY RESCALE-TO-TARGET" BOARD IS INCOHERENT BY ARCHITECTURE, NOT BY BUG — and PERMUTING A COMPOSITE QUANTITY REDISTRIBUTES EVERY FACTOR INSIDE IT (NF-INJ1, 2026-08-21):** the NFL season board builds `line = rate × games`, then a downstream ordering step (NF1.5) permutes the within-position POINT multiset and rescales the stat line to the new point WITHOUT moving games (`_RAW_SCALE_COLS` omits `proj_games`) — so a low-availability player promoted in the multiset gets his line ×up-to-3.5 with games FROZEN (10 physically-impossible rows, all QB). Because MVP-1's point EMBEDS availability, permuting it as pure QUALITY redistributes the injury DISCOUNT: +36.4% of the cut handed BACK on 23 injury-capped players, 18/23 scaled UP, landing on DRAFTABLE players (Kittle ×1.29, Pierce ×1.67, Higgins ×2.32) — the injury priority runs BACKWARDS. ⭐ The weekly stack (NF-W7c `fp_assembly`) is built the OPPOSITE way (stats are the primitive, drawn jointly, points DERIVED) and is coherent BY CONSTRUCTION. **RULES: (1)** a rescale-to-a-point-target board's incoherence is ARCHITECTURAL — it RECURS with every new point-level step (NF1.5 ordering, TR2b/D16 level constants each move the implied per-game rate up to ~25%, rookie `fp_target` allocation — FOUR steps move the point without the games; they STACK and nobody has graded the composition), so the durable cure is a coherent generator (NF-W8 unification), not per-step patches; **(2)** PERMUTING/REMAPPING A COMPOSITE quantity redistributes EVERY factor inside it — applies to ANY rank-remap in the program, not just fantasy; **(3)** when two served quantities are each defensible but their RATIO is not (point vs games → an impossible per-game rate), GUARD THE RATIO — which is what let a guard ship AHEAD of the model fix instead of waiting on it; **(4)** a DERIVED SERVING ARTIFACT that becomes a PAID substrate (the stat line, NF-EPIC1's 'crown jewel') has typically NEVER been a bake-off target — every study graded points/ordering — so it carries ungraded defects until something finally measures it. ⭐ SIBLING (the same staleness class NF-INJ1 fixed, still open on a sibling input): a HEALTHY feed with a STALE board — the injury Delta was 15.4h fresh while the board's copy was 26 DAYS old, and NF-FRESH2 had stamped the correct `sleeper_status_as_of` the whole time but NOTHING read it back; the depth chart feeds `expected_games` and is 6.9d stale on the same board (an ingest-ran-but-not-rebuilt / INC-25 check). A stamp nothing reads back is not a freshness guarantee.
- **🧷 A REPRODUCTION PIN CANNOT CATCH A DEFECT THE PARENT AND CHILD SHARE — a pin can faithfully reproduce a BUG (NCAAF-CLV-repair, 2026-08-22):** VAL1's §2a reproduction pin passed CLEANLY on the pre-repair run — it "reproduced the recorded S1-serve CLV EXACTLY" — because BOTH sides ran the same misaligned `reset_index` code, so the pin was faithfully reproducing a bug. A reproduction pin proves a child matches its parent; it says NOTHING about whether the shared computation is CORRECT. ⇒ a reproduction pin is necessary-not-sufficient: pair it with an independent CORRECTNESS check that does NOT go through the shared code path (here: side-agreement with `sign(μ−close)` at the rate the Gaussian predictive arithmetically requires, ~0.98). ⭐ SIBLING (the same story) — **an AGGREGATE headline can be BYTE-INVARIANT to a defect that flips half the underlying rows, so a before/after DIFF of the headline says "nothing changed" and is WRONG:** the ATS leg landed on EXACTLY 2,039/4,110 under BOTH the broken and repaired reads while **45.8% of the underlying sides flipped** (and S1-serve's O/U 0.5127 coincidentally matched on another vintage too) — so the discriminating statistic must be the mechanism-level one (side-agreement), never the aggregate hit rate. Both are the row-misalignment class: a broken eval is guaranteed a null AND a stable-looking aggregate, so neither the value nor its stability can certify the pipeline.
- **🧭 A LEARNER SELECTED TO ORDER QUANTITY A CANNOT BE HANDED A MULTISET IN UNIT B — coherence and ordering are NOT jointly satisfiable with a learner fitted on the wrong quantity, and the damage RANKS WITH the substituted signal's per-group deficit (NF-INJ2, 2026-08-22):** NF1.5's ordering learner is fitted on `real_fp_ppr` (a season TOTAL), so it orders POINTS. NF-INJ2's coherence fix permuted the per-game RATE and re-multiplied by games — which hands that points-fitted ranker a RATE multiset, a question it was never validated on. Result: it fixed coherence + the injury give-back + won CRPS, but DEGRADED the draftable-tier QB ordering (ρ 0.481→0.350, BH-significant) ⇒ refused (and correctly not overridden — the breached constraint is a product metric a drafter uses, unlike NF-W8's deflation gate). ⭐ The damage ranked EXACTLY with the games-signal's per-position deficit (worst at QB, where expected-games is the weakest ordering signal beside the learned score; TE actually GAINED, where games is relatively strongest). **RULE: a coherence/reformulation fix that changes the UNIT a downstream ranker consumes is NOT free — the ranker must be RE-SELECTED/RE-FIT on the new target, or coherence and ordering won't hold together; and a per-group metric degrades most where the substituted signal is weakest.** ⛔ a per-group (e.g. QB-only) rescue is inadmissible unless PRE-REGISTERED FORWARD (adopting it after seeing the per-position damage = discovering a family after scoring = the selection bias DSR deflates). Sibling of the point-primitive/rescale-to-target coherence class (NF-INJ1): the durable cure is a coherent generator whose ranker is validated on the quantity it's asked to order (NF-INJ2b), not a per-step patch.
- **🧮 TRIMMING A HIGH-PBO FIELD TO THE ELIGIBLE SET DOES NOT ALWAYS RESCUE THE NULL — the eligible-set PBO can be WORSE, and the discriminating measurement is the TWO-ARM decision, which is what makes a single-contrast successor ADMISSIBLE rather than an assumption (NCAAF-VAL3, 2026-08-22):** the standing PBO guidance (NF1.8) says compute PBO over the eligible set, and the tempting reading of a high PBO with ineligible nulls in the field is "the nulls inflate it — trim them and it clears." VAL3 MEASURED the opposite: pre-registered 8-arm PBO 0.530, eligible-set (5-arm) **0.701 (WORSE)**, only the **TWO-ARM decision** (foil vs the one correction) reaches 0.000. ⇒ a high PBO from FOUR near-identical correction FORMS is the selection statistic answering "which form?" when the decision is "whether to correct?" (NF1.8's tied-field signature) — and the fix is NOT a field trim (MH2.2) but a FRESH single-contrast re-registration that makes PBO INAPPLICABLE (the `classify_null` n_arms<2 branch), the remaining gates already clearing. **RULE: before calling a high-PBO null "genuine overfitting," compute PBO over the pre-registered field, the eligible set, AND the two-arm decision; if only the two-arm collapses to ~0 while the eligible set is no better, the binding issue is FIELD COMPOSITION (near-clones), and a single coherent contrast registered FORWARD is admissible — the MEASUREMENT (not an assumption) is what distinguishes this from the NF-W7f "assumed the field did it" trap.** ⚠️ still read the whole-field spread as measuring the NULLS not the contest (NF1.8), and ⛔ decide whether the estimator under-corrects by a PAIRED read, never the leaderboard rank (VAL3's `over_scale` topped the rank but the paired test was a tie — a rank can't tell a tie from a win).
- **🗂️ A FIXED-OUTPUT-PATH WRITE IN A STAGE A LATER STORY REUSES SILENTLY CLOBBERS THE PRIOR STORY'S AUDIT TRAIL, AND A REPORT RENDERS ONLY THE CURRENT INVOCATION'S OUTPUT — neither raises (NCAAF-P2.1 S1-serve, 2026-08-17; defects 3+4, same class):** (1) finalize under a NEW `strength_pace` contract OVERWROTE `ncaaf_p1_4_calibration.{json,md}` — a DECIDED story's audit trail — with a different contract's run (caught by `git status` pre-commit, not by any test). (2) the board publish SILENTLY DELETED the calibration section from the P1.5 report: a board-only run computes NO calibration, the report renders only what THAT invocation produced, the path is fixed → the routine publish wiped the story's own gate evidence EVERY time, no error (found only by a real `--s3` run). RULES: a post-decision contract/story writes to its OWN output paths (never the decided story's); a report re-renders un-recomputed sections from the PERSISTED JSON stamped '⏳ NOT recomputed by this run — computed <ISO>' so staleness is VISIBLE not inferable. ⭐ SIBLING (guard) — **an EQUALITY/AGREEMENT guard between two callers of a shared function proves they AGREE, not that they're RIGHT — pair it with a clause pinning the ARITHMETIC (the E9.61 two-renderers lesson, guard edition):** S1-serve's RED-proof found 'the shared derivation is wrong' stayed GREEN because breaking the shared `derive_pace_composites` moved BOTH callers together (the equality still held); and 'simulate_season ignores pace' stayed GREEN because the board-level control was satisfied by the CCG/bracket legs ALONE (a dead regular-season draw would pass) → pin each leg (standings-level + neutral-draw) separately, and pin the VALUE not just the agreement. ⭐ SIBLING (serving pattern worth copying) — **serve a mean model as a COEFFICIENT TABLE, not a pickle, + a contract-match guard + a NULL-inertness proof:** S1-serve made μ an `ncaaf_game_mean_v2.json` coefficient table (ordered columns + impute vector + scaler + ridge coefs) — version-proof + diffable + PR-reviewable, dodging the MLB unpinned-sklearn `_loss` pickle landmine; `load_served_pair()` RAISES if the dispersion and mean were fitted on DIFFERENT contracts (an E7.9 train/serve mismatch can't be assembled by accident); and because StandardScaler mean-imputation makes a missing feature contribute EXACTLY 0.0 (bit-for-bit — `μ = intercept + Σ coef·(x−mean)/scale`, `mean_` = the NaN fill), a pre-season board with 100%-NULL pace is PROVABLY byte-identical — the safety argument that let an in-season-only effect deploy mid-August (asserted 3 ways: artifact math, end-to-end, real board). Also: a leg that SCORES A SEASON needs an unplayed-season guard (the conf-title leg scored 2026's 136 teams as label-0 'nobody won' the moment they entered the mart — a pure artefact indistinguishable from a regression; the natty leg already had the guard, the conf leg didn't).
- **🎛️ WHEN A REAL EFFECT FAILS DSR INSIDE A HETEROGENEOUS BATTERY, COMPUTE THE 2×2 (SERIES × FIELD) BEFORE NAMING THE REMEDY — FIELD COHERENCE CHANGES `V`, THE RETURN-SERIES CHOICE IS USUALLY SECONDARY (NCAAF-P2.1 S1, 2026-08-17; the MH2.5/NF-W6b-C `V`-inflation mechanism, now isolated with a decomposition):** P2.1 diagnosed `pace`'s DSR failure (0.041) as a RETURN-SERIES fault (PBO wants many buckets, DSR wants low-noise per-fold obs) and prescribed "separate the series." S1 re-registered with the series split AND a coherently-declared 3-arm pace family, shipped (DSR 0.998), then a post-verdict 2×2 OVERTURNED the story's premise: per-fold series ALONE under P2.1's 16-arm field → DSR 0.39 (the 0/8-fold losers carry large negative per-fold Sharpes → `V` 0.68 → SR0 1.60 > pace's SR 1.49); the coherent 3-arm family ALONE cleared even on P2.1's ORIGINAL bucket series → 0.98. ⇒ **FIELD COHERENCE was the primary lever; the series was secondary.** RULE: `classify_null`'s `max_field_size=0` / DSR_UNREACHABLE is computed at the BATTERY's `V` — a coherent PRE-REGISTERED family (MH2(a)) changes `V`, not `N`, so a battery-level DSR_UNREACHABLE is correct for its OWN field but does NOT mean the effect is dead; re-register the effect's own coherent family FORWARD and recompute `V`. ⛔ still no post-hoc trim (MH2.2) — the family is DECLARED, and S1 disclosed the per-fold Sharpe was already public from P2.1 rather than crediting a "new" series. ⭐ SIBLING (where the effect lives, for the successor): the 2-col composites `{pace_sum,pace_diff}` beat the 8-col block by 0.018 (six per-side levels cost via ratio-identity + ridge redundancy) and `pace_sum` alone ≈ the composites → the effect is the POSSESSIONS channel on the TOTAL axis, ~zero margin content; the primary shipped as registered (the prereg forbade "pick the best of three") and the composite is successor S1b.
- **🪟 A PER-FORM PEEKING-ORACLE FLOOR READS AN *INACTIVE* ANCHOR PAIR AS A *REFUSAL* — a shippable arm dies on a gate that couldn't fire (NF-W6d, 2026-08-17; the NF-D20 'count the folds the mechanism can act on' + NF-D16 (g‴) lineage, now on the W6b-C `winner_own_form_floor`):** three arms (QB|carries +16%, QB|fumbles_lost +5%, RB|targets +12%; all 8/8 folds, PBO 0, DSR ≥ 0.998) passed EVERY statistical gate and were CONSTRAINT_REFUSED by ONE clause — the winner's OWN-form peeking oracle TIED its matched-n control (≤ 0.0015 CRPS), which the floor reads as 'the form has no headroom' though the anchor pair is really INACTIVE (the peek had nothing to act on at that cell). RULE: a per-form oracle floor that TIES (not LOSES to) its matched control is INACTIVE, not a refusal — before crediting a `*_floor` refusal, check the anchor pair could ACT (a nonzero peek−control gap); an inactive anchor is UNINFORMATIVE (NF-D20), never a fail. Left recorded-not-relabelled per E2.1-r; a successor registers a matched-pair tie AS inactive, and the fix belongs in the SHARED harness gate (recurs on any per-form-floor §0.5 cell). ⭐ SIBLING — **a real effect can SHIP with DSR WAIVED when the DSR failure is a declared-but-far-out arm inflating cross-trial dispersion, remedy = a fresh coherent registration never a trim (NF-W6d QB|attempts):** the hurdle beat head+bank +17.4% (8/8, PBO 0) but DSR 0.806 because a declared far-out `count_negbin` arm (Sharpe −5.5) inflated `V` (the MH2.5/NF-W6b-C heterogeneous-field mechanism) and the declared field of 4 admits no trim (MH2.2) → it SHIPS DSR-waived, and the durable remedy is a fresh attempts family WITHOUT the NB2 arm (mechanistically justified: attempts is BIMODAL — starter/backup — so an NB2 around one mean CANNOT express it; the PIT-decile screen rejected NB2 at 0.273, correctly — the 'choose a default by calibration not CRPS' discipline doing real work). ⭐ SIBLING (process) — **size a §0.5 runtime from a COUNT-class fold (arms × LGBM fits), not an event-stat proof:** Phase B ran 3.6 h vs a ~1.5 h quote because count-class cells carry far more fitting than the event-stat smoke used to estimate it. And a CLOSED cell can RE-OPEN under a different form — three receiving-TD cells NF-W6 closed at 0.07–0.38% re-gated at 2.7–7% under ATOM-AWARE forms (the 'different mechanism' closure explicitly asked for).
- **📏 THE NF-W6 ORACLE-FIRST METHOD DOES NOT LITERALLY TRANSFER TO A SMALL-BLOCK CELL — a same-family peek at block-n can be CAPACITY-STARVED, so a NEGATIVE 'ceiling' is NOT evidence of no headroom (NF-W7i, 2026-08-18):** the block-only cross-fit peek scored 2.6495 vs the arm's 2.4692 — a NEGATIVE 7.3% 'ceiling' — purely because K=3 left the peek ~721 rows to fit against an arm trained on 13k-20.6k (W7h's §2 predicted this). RULES: (a) check the peek's ACTIVITY against an effective-n control (NF-W6d/NF-D20) — block-only was ACTIVE (beat its own n-control 2.6495 vs 2.687), so the negative ceiling is the CAPACITY gap, not a dead peek; (b) BRACKET with a train∪block peek at weight λ whose control swaps the peeked block for an equally-sized, equally-weighted slice of RECENT TRAIN rows — without it a λ=1 peek is indistinguishable from plain RECENCY WEIGHTING; the λ monotonicity (λ=1 +0.54% → λ=20 −5.13%) proves the negative ceiling is STARVATION and makes λ=1 the ceiling-MAXIMISING setting (so every declared bias favours BUILD and a NO is conservative). ⭐ THE HEADLINE peek W7h carried as a '39.5% ceiling gap' was a ROW-LEVEL in-sample degenerate (`fit_direct_points(te_p, te_p)` — every row sees its own label = the zero-CRPS degenerate NF-W6 rules out); W7h only ever asked an arm to BEAT it (gated nothing), but it CANNOT carry a ceiling claim — never re-import an in-sample peek as a ceiling. ⭐ SIBLING (classify_null answers the WRONG question for a materiality gate): it answers 'effect > 0?' and returned POWER_LIMITED ('buy more seasons') for a ceiling whose whole 95% CI lies BELOW the pre-registered band — the actively-misleading NF-D18/MH2(g″) direction. For a band/materiality decision an interval WHOLLY BELOW the band is DECISIVE (MEASURED_IMMATERIAL), not underpowered; REPORT THE CI IN BAND UNITS so the comparison is a direct read (a 4th cv_power defect, carded). ⭐ SIBLING (a failing POSITIVE CONTROL may indict its own STATISTIC, not the instrument): the ×1.3-shift control failed (ceiling moved 0.821 vs a ≥2.0 bar) — but a MULTIPLICATIVE shift inflates BOTH the numerator AND denominator of a RELATIVE ceiling, understating sensitivity by construction; the instrument WAS sensitive (×3.9 absolute response). Diagnose a failing positive control before concluding blindness, and make the refusal WRITE ITS EVIDENCE before it raises or the diagnosis costs a full re-run (E2.1-r class, one level up); the amended clause requires the ABSOLUTE advantage grow ≥2× AND the ceiling widen (a design quantity, amended FORWARD, original retained + reported FAILING).
- **📐 AN ORACLE-FIRST CEILING GATE WITH MATCHED-N CONTROLS SEPARATES *FORM* HEADROOM FROM *INFORMATION* HEADROOM — and its bands must refuse real-but-immaterial ceilings (NF-W6, 2026-08-14):** after 3 consecutive component nulls (W3/W4/W5), NF-W6 did NOT open with a bake-off — it measured the per-cell improvement CEILING first (block-peeking oracles, cross-fit K=3 so no row sees its own label, + a MATCHED-N control = the same candidate form trained honestly at block size with NO peek), and built only where the ceiling was large. ⭐ THE MATCHED-N CONTROL IS THE ATTRIBUTION: a control landing WITHIN NOISE of the peeking oracle says the gap is FORM (fix the predictive SHAPE — here the champion under-priced the zero atom + expressed no conditional spread), NOT information (no new features implied); a control far BELOW the oracle would say the gap needs a signal you don't have. RULES: (a) run an oracle-first ceiling gate before a bake-off whenever the prior is a likely null — a small ceiling is a complete result ('already near the ceiling') at a fraction of a bake-off's cost; (b) a load-bearing oracle must be SAME-FAMILY as the candidate (NF-W6 added a 9-knot quantile oracle because the position-constant-bank oracles are structurally BLIND to conditional heteroscedasticity — omitting it risked a false NO); (c) the ceiling BANDS must refuse a ceiling that is statistically DEMONSTRABLE but IMMATERIAL (NF-W6's 4 TD-NO cells were real at 0.07-0.38% and correctly refused — the mirror image of 'underpowered ≠ absent': 'demonstrable ≠ material'); (d) a peek must be the block's EFFICIENCY REGIME, not the answers — a row-level peek is a zero-CRPS degenerate, not a ceiling (cross-fit within the block). ⭐ THE PROGRAM FINDING: the per-player predictive SHAPE is where value lives (per-stat 7-17%, BIGGER than MARGIN's sub-1% points-tail refinements, because per-stat there is NO distributional incumbent at all — only point means), while the environment/availability/correlation channels are inert.
- **📐 CHECK A CANDIDATE GATE STATISTIC FOR ARM-INVARIANCE *BEFORE* REGISTERING IT — a tail-only construction can't move the beyond-champion-grid PIT mass, so gating on it ships a STRUCTURALLY-INACTIVE clause (NF-MARGIN2, 2026-08-13; the NF-D20 class caught at DESIGN time):** the natural calibration gate for a tail fix — beyond-champion-grid PIT mass vs nominal — is ARM-INVARIANT for a tail-ONLY arm (the extension redistributes mass WITHIN the tail, it cannot change how often reality exceeds q975), so gating on it would have registered an inactive clause. RULE: before registering a gate statistic, prove the arm can actually MOVE it — a statistic the arm cannot change is not a gate, it's décor. The fix read the beyond-EVAL-grid ends (nominal 0.005/side) via a levels-generalized PIT — ⭐ **the native 39-level `crps_q39`/PIT instrument is PROVABLY BLIND to any beyond-grid tail work** (it quantizes at the grid), so any future beyond-grid tail/interval study needs `randomized_pit_levels`, not the 39-level default. SIBLING (verdict-reading): a `CONSTRAINT_REFUSED` on a magnitude anchor (`over_ext` ×3 registered to lose; a permutation edging the aligned arm) means the ESTIMATOR under-extends, ⛔ NOT that the effect is absent — NF-MARGIN2's tail channel was real + clean at all 4 positions while QB/WR refused; more folds can't fix a magnitude estimator (a successor pre-registers a BETTER estimator, NOT a re-run).
- **🎚️ WHEN A POINT PROJECTION IS SYSTEMATICALLY LOW BUT ITS BAND IS RIGHT, RECALIBRATE THE POINT ONLY — SCALING THE BAND WITH THE POINT MANUFACTURES A SPURIOUS λ OPTIMUM (NF-TR2, 2026-08-15; explains + supersedes NF-B3 / NF-RECAL1 / NF-C3-REREAD):** the served fantasy band already sat at the realized level (`knn_norm` = q10/q90 of realized outcomes of similarly-projected players); only the POINT was low (the incumbent point sat at ~0.46 of its OWN band). NF-B3/RECAL1 scaled the band WITH the point (`apply_to_band`), which is exactly why they found a CRPS optimum at λ≈0.5 — a band artifact, not a real interior optimum. With the band held FIXED, CRPS improves MONOTONICALLY through a full mean-match, so the fix is a POINT-ONLY move that leaves the NF1.9-validated interval byte-identical. RULE: before fitting a λ that scales an interval, check whether the BAND is already calibrated and only the POINT is off — a λ that co-moves a correct band invents a spurious optimum. ⭐ SIBLING — **a FULL-HISTORY mean-match OVER-corrects when the level is NON-STATIONARY, and the no-inflation gate catches it → the fix is a better ESTIMATOR under a fresh registration, never a λ (NF-TR2b; the MARGIN2→3 / NF-W6b-C CONSTRAINT_REFUSED→successor pattern, Nth instance):** the full-history fit was CONSTRAINT_REFUSED by its own pre-registered no-inflation gate (2007–09 is a different regime; QB has run hot since 2019 → pooled OOF bias flipped to +7.88); the successor used a trailing window DERIVED from the data (smallest window giving every position ≥90 tier rows = 5, pinned + RAISE-checked, not tuned) and SHIPPED. ⭐ SIBLING — **an AFFINE recalibration's rank-safety is CONDITIONAL: a negative fitted slope inverts a whole position's board (NF-D16), measured on 6 of 13 folds here — the affine foil won raw CRPS and was correctly C1-REFUSED.** Guard within-position order per fold; never assume an affine preserves rank. ⭐ SIBLING (honest-framing) — **a HEDGE that names only the 'even' cases silently VANISHES when a case crosses into 'behind' — a hedge that disappears as the evidence gets WORSE:** the track-record lead named only even-ranked positions, so when RB crossed to 'the crowd's order was slightly better than ours' the hedge above it disappeared → make the derived hedge SYMMETRIC (name a behind position too). Two process reconfirmations: WIRED ≠ INVOKED (the build-time level fit shipped a NameError; 26 tests, none INVOKED the block with the policy ON → the operator's real rebuild found it — a guard must run the block ON), and a REPRODUCTION-PIN hard-coded to a headline artifact must be RE-PINNED in the same change as a deliberate headline regeneration AND the exporter must REFUSE a VOID/failed-anchor run (a VOID interval could otherwise publish beside a fresh scorecard).
- **📐 A QUANTILE-LEARNER PREDICTIVE THAT ENDS AT THE 5/95 KNOTS + EXTENDS FLAT HAS NO TAILS — a SERVING-REPRESENTATION defect invisible to a coarse eval grid (NF-MARGIN1, 2026-08-11):** the fantasy champion's quantile learners fit 9 knots ending at 0.05/0.95 and `interp_to_levels` extended them FLAT to the grid ends, so the SERVED predictive literally has no tails — beyond-grid PIT mass 1.6-2.1× nominal, `cov95 ≡ cov99` (the flat-tail fingerprint), while the 50% central interval OVER-covers 13-21pp (a KURTOSIS defect: tails too thin AND shoulders too wide — NOT a variance/'just widen' defect; the clamped widen-only arm pinned at w=1.0 with nothing admissible to do). This was the true cause of NF-W5's team-total under-coverage (0.706), NOT missing correlation. RULES: (a) any distributional target on a knot-quantile + flat-extension representation inherits this tail truncation — carry a TAIL-MODEL requirement (exponential mean-excess beyond the grid) from day one (NF-W6/W7 inherit it); (b) a tail fix is INVISIBLE to the native 39-level eval grid — score tail work on a dense grid (`crps_q199`) or the instrument can't see the repair; (c) fit any post-hoc recalibration on a PURGED calibration slice — in-sample PITs of a boosted model are optimistically flat. Reusable machinery: `margin_calibration.py` (199-level grid, PIT recal, exponential-tail extension).
- **🎯 A QUANTILE-GRID CRPS SILENTLY TIES ARMS ON A BINARY / TWO-POINT TARGET — use the EXACT closed form (NF-W4, 2026-08-11):** `crps_q39` scores on a 39-level quantile grid that quantizes a probability to 0.025 steps, so two arms whose P(event) differ by <0.025 score IDENTICALLY — a silent tie that hides the real winner of a P(event) bake-off. NF-W4 declared up front that T1 (P(played)) is scored by the EXACT Bernoulli CRPS (`crps_bernoulli`, the dense-grid limit of `crps_q39`) + `crps_point` for a two-point target, with a reducer that REFUSES a non-finite predictive. RULE: for any binary / two-point / P(event) target, score with the exact closed-form CRPS, never a coarse quantile grid — the grid-quantization tie is a trap every future P(event) bake-off would otherwise hit (the reducers are reusable in the vertical). SIBLING (recurring): `cv_power.classify_null` at `n_arms=1` again rendered a one-contrast design as a FOLD shortage ('−4 more folds' at 8 folds) — the 4TH hand-correction in this vertical (NF-W2 → NF-D18 → NF-W3 → NF-W4); the derive-not-store verdict layer makes each correction free, which is why it keeps shipping uncaught → fixing the instrument itself is now carded.
- **🃏 TWO SILENT-POPULATION-SHRINK DEFECTS IN A COMPONENT BAKE-OFF (NF-W3, 2026-08-09):** (a) **`pbp.posteam` uses CURRENT franchise codes while schedules/weekly_rosters keep the ERA code** (pbp LAC/LV vs SD/OAK through 2019) → the join NaN'd exactly the relocated franchises, and because pandas `merge` treats NaN keys as matchable, those rows CROSS-JOINED into duplicates (34 rows in a 32-team week). Surfaced ONLY because the NF-W0a PIT guard refused all 65 affected weeks / 1,994 rows fail-closed (NF-W1 never saw it — pbp is the odd source out). RULE: joining pbp to schedule/roster data needs a franchise-code normalization (era→current) BEFORE the join; a join key that can go NaN on both sides is a duplicate-row bomb — assert row-count conservation across any team-grain join. (b) **`scipy.stats.binom`/`betabinom` return NaN for a NON-INTEGER `n`** — a 4-game rolling trial count is non-integer almost always, so both share-GLM arms emitted NaN on most rows and `np.nanmean` SILENTLY scored them on a smaller population than the rest of the field (betabinom 0.0626→0.0574 after the fix — from losing the foil to beating it). RULE: a leaderboard whose arms are scored on DIFFERENT populations is not a comparison — the CRPS reducer must REFUSE a non-finite predictive, never nan-mean past it (a non-vacuity assertion on the fixture catches it). SIBLING (3rd correction of `cv_power.classify_null` in this vertical): at an honest `n_arms=1` a single pre-registered contrast has NO search to overfit, so `pbo_evaluable` is false — but the instrument RENDERED that as a FOLD shortage ("8 folds < 4", prescribing "−4 more folds") → a trigger telling a future reader to buy seasons for a null no season count can move. Report `pbo` UNDEFINED (not failed) for a single contrast; a classify_null fold-shortage trigger on a 1-arm gate is SUSPECT. And the NF-W2e verdict-derived-not-stored rule rises one level — to the null STATE (`--rewrite-report` re-derives states from stored per-fold selections + stamps `verdict_corrected_from`), so fixing a mislabelled state costs zero refit.
- **🔬 THREE §0.5 FINDINGS FROM THE 2025 INJURY RE-GATE (NF-W2d/W2e, 2026-08-09):** (a) ⭐ **A STRATIFIER-VALIDATION CAN ITSELF BE CONFOUNDED, AND IT FAILS TOWARD A FALSE `INACTIVE` — the most closable-looking verdict there is (MH2.1's validate-the-stratifier lesson, one level up).** NF-W2e's first cut read "capture age separates nothing" (rank corr −0.003, p=0.947) and would have closed the study — an ARTIFACT: nfl.com publishes practice-only rows with a NULL `report_status`, so a pair of captures crossing a designation-source↔practice-source boundary scored as a "change" that was a SOURCE-FORMAT difference, not a content change (286/386 pairs). Restricted to pairs where the compared field is genuinely populated on BOTH sides (n=341): rank corr +0.289, p<1e-4. RULE: on a MULTI-SOURCE panel, restrict a stratifier-validation comparison to rows where the compared field is real on both sides, and PUBLISH BOTH CUTS — a source-format boundary masquerading as a content boundary is the default risk, and its failure mode is a false INACTIVE (the most convincing "close the story" verdict). (b) ⭐ **A VERDICT SENTENCE MUST BE THREE-WAY AND DERIVED, NOT TWO-WAY AND STORED.** A BEATS/LOSES word cannot express a TIE and narrated NF-W2e's real `inj_freshest` tie as a LOSS — inverting the whole carry-over conclusion off CORRECT arithmetic. Fix: three-way (BEATS/TIES/LOSES) failing closed to TIES, with the sentence DERIVED at report time (⛔ not stored at scoring time, or correcting it costs a full refit — the pressure that leaves a known-wrong sentence in a published record). (c) **THE AS-OF-CAPTURE (SERVED-ERA) EFFECT OF A PIT-CAPTURED FEATURE IS SMALLER THAN A VENDOR-STAMPED BACKTEST — quote the served-era magnitude for planning.** The 2025 as-of-capture era priced the injury lift ~⅓ of the legacy vendor-stamped figure at RB (+0.042 vs +0.135); roadmap arithmetic sized on a vendor-stamped backtest of an archive-captured feature is ~2-3× optimistic. ⛔ do NOT reflexively tighten a freshness bound after seeing a freshness gradient — NF-W2e MEASURED that (drop to ≤1d) and it LOSES (a stale designation < fresh but > none); the lever is CAPTURE CADENCE (gameday-close), not filtering.
- **📏 A LOAD-BEARING NUMBER QUOTED IN N PLACES BUT EXTRAPOLATED FROM ONE UN-RE-TAKEN MEASUREMENT IS A PHANTOM UNTIL RE-MEASURED — and IDEMPOTENCE is a weak proxy for a transform's CORRECTNESS (ESPN-PRUNER, 2026-08-14):** the ESPN pruner's justification — 'un-pruned is 3.3 MB so a 12-team league sits at ~99% of the 4 MB cap and a 14-team is REFUSED' — was quoted in 3 docstrings and drove a 'launch risk' card, all extrapolated from ONE measurement nobody re-took. A real drafted capture measured **834 KB / 20.9% of cap** — the figure was ~4× wrong and the risk was a PHANTOM (the pruner is a real 6.4× reduction, kept on merit). RULE: before treating a cited number as a CONSTRAINT (a cap %, a latency, a row count, a credit/day, a MAU %), RE-MEASURE the real artifact — a figure repeated across N files is not N pieces of evidence, it's one un-re-taken measurement cited N times. SIBLING: the pruner's server invariant had been tested only as IDEMPOTENCE ('pruning twice == pruning once') — but a pruner deleting the WRONG subtree is flawlessly idempotent while destroying the league; idempotence is a weak proxy for a transform's CORRECTNESS → test the transform DIRECTLY against a real RAW input.
- **🧪 A FIXTURE THAT IS THE POST-TRANSFORM ARTIFACT CANNOT TEST THE TRANSFORM — capture the RAW input, not the cleaned output (E9.64b, 2026-08-14):** the ESPN import's `pruneEspnPayload` strips a real drafted response 3.3MB→147KB (without it a 12-team league hits ~99% of the 4MB request cap and a 14-team is REFUSED — the two commonest sizes on the biggest platform), and its failure mode is SILENT (`catch { return text }` returns the original on any unexpected shape). But all three committed ESPN captures are the ALREADY-PRUNED artifact (zero occurrences of every field the pruner removes), so nothing tested that it prunes — the E2E was the first run of the pruner, and on a payload with nothing to prune. RULE: to test a transform/cleaner/pruner, the fixture must be the RAW pre-transform input; a fixture derived from the transform's OWN output is a tautology (sibling of NF-C0e's 'a fixture from the first payload can't disconfirm a wrong key-map' + the red-proof-break-that-no-longer-bites family). SIBLING (same story): a `platform === 'yahoo' ? 'Yahoo' : 'Sleeper'` two-way test on a THREE-way field read every ESPN import back as 'Sleeper' on the one screen whose job is confirming what we parsed — `tsc` is happy (both branches are strings) and nothing had opened that screen on a non-Sleeper preview; an incomplete branch on an enum is the NF-C0e wrong-key class on the DISPLAY side.
- **🧪 A RED PROOF WRAPPING A `pytest.raises` CLAUSE MUST CATCH `BaseException`, NOT `Exception` — pytest's `Failed` derives from `BaseException`, so a deliberate break sails STRAIGHT THROUGH and the RED proof reports SUCCESS (NF-W6c, 2026-08-15):** two NF-W6c RED proofs initially "passed" a break they never caught — the inner `pytest.raises` raised `Failed` (a `BaseException` subclass), the outer `except Exception` didn't catch it, so the mutation went green. The vacuous-guard family (NF1.7(a)/INC-38/E9.64) at the test-of-the-test level: a RED proof that structurally cannot fail. RULE: any RED proof asserting a clause that itself uses `pytest.raises` catches `BaseException`. ⭐ SIBLING (planning, not a bug): a per-stat LGBM distributional harness is near-FLAT in train size (~57 fixed 250-tree fits + a per-serve-row loop, NOT O(train rows)) → 5× the data cost 1.05× the wall-clock (measured 170s, not the ~13min a linear-in-rows extrapolation predicted); ⛔ don't size a build's runtime linearly in training rows. And the drift-proofing pattern worth reusing when a §0.5 bake-off certifies a CONSTRUCTION but ships NO pickle (so serving must re-fit): make the serving module DISPATCH-ONLY into the certified arm modules — import no learner (AST-checked), let no fit/predict verb reach its code, assert dispatch targets by identity — so a second full-train fit cannot silently diverge from the certified one.
- **🧪 A RED-PROOF (mutation) TEST IS ONLY AS DURABLE AS THE DATA-FLOW ITS BREAK ASSUMES — and a red proof that lives in NO workflow silently rots (E9.64, 2026-08-14):** E9.64 found 6 pre-existing red-proof cases whose verdict no longer matched their declaration. FOUR failure modes, all reusable: (1) ⭐ **when a READ MOVES, every case whose break depends on that read failing quietly STOPS working** — NF-EPIC1 moved scoring server-side, so a break that mutated the client scorer stopped changing anything (the assertion was fine; the BREAK died). (2) **re-anchoring a test onto a new quantity ORPHANS the old anchor's reader** — E9.61 re-anchored the movers test onto VOR and left the overall-rank-move column read by NOTHING, so inverting its sign stayed green (a real coverage hole); when a spec's anchor moves, check what the PREVIOUS anchor was the only reader of. (3) **a red proof for a RACE must FORCE the race** — a harness that answers RED then MISMATCH on identical code is worse than one that fails, because a single green reads as proof; order the reads with an explicit ordering tool (⛔ documented as ordering, NOT a latency knob, so it can't later paper over a race). (4) ⭐ **THE RED PROOF RAN IN NO CI WORKFLOW AT ALL — the mechanism by which all 6 went dead unnoticed.** A mutation/red-proof suite that isn't scheduled carries less information than it appears to, and 'the E2E suite is green' decays silently. RULE: schedule the red-proof (nightly/weekly is enough) with a signal when its RED-count moves; a mutation test off the CI schedule is decorative. Sibling of the vacuous-guard family (NF1.7(a)/INC-38): a guard that cannot fail — here because its break no longer bites, or because nothing runs it — is not a guard.
- **🗄️ A USER'S ENTIRE STATE LIVES IN ONE DynamoDB ITEM SHARING THE 400 KB CAP — SO A PER-FIELD CAP IS NOT AN ITEM BUDGET, AND AN OVERFLOW BRICKS EVERY WRITE FOR THAT USER (NF-C6P3, 2026-08-15):** all of a user's fantasy leagues + portfolio + platform tokens + scoring ledger + MLB leagues are ONE item; a 25-league quota × a 14-team roster set exceeds 400 KB on its own, and DynamoDB refuses the WHOLE `UpdateItem` on overflow → after that, no new league, no bet, no preference save — the user is silently bricked. RULES: (a) a growable per-league/per-field cap is NOT sufficient — add a TOTAL item budget checked BEFORE the write (NF-C6P3: 260 KB of 400), and drop only the INCOMING write's data (first-come), never evict other data on a write the user experiences as saving one thing; (b) truncate by whole ENTITIES (whole teams), never partial (a half-stored roster produces a total that's quietly too low and looks real; a missing team is countable + nameable); (c) record the truncation on a served flag so the product degrades visibly, not silently. ⭐ **SIBLING — the D/ST franchise join (the NF-W3 franchise-code / NF-C0e wrong-key family, on team defenses):** our board renders a defense as `DET D/ST` while every platform uses the nickname (`Lions D/ST` / `Detroit Lions` / `Detroit`) → the normalized NAME join matched 0 of 15 defenses on every platform, EVER, and because a miss is a legit `board: null` the slot silently showed 'nobody eligible' with its points missing from the headline total. Join on FRANCHISE (row `team` → nickname → abbreviation token), ⛔ NO fuzzy fallback (a wrong-defense match credits undrafted points = worse than the miss). Pin the nickname map against the repo's franchise authority (`coaching_source._FRANCHISE_ERAS`) — that anchor found the two-word 'Washington Football Team' 2020-21 gap. ⭐ **SIBLING — vacuous guards, again, all caught by RED-proofs not a green suite:** (1) an E2E clause passed with the tested filter DELETED because the MOCK FIXTURE (rivals holding a mid-board slice) decided the answer, not the assertion → make the fixture unable to satisfy the assertion by accident (rivals draft best-remaining); (2) a slimming test compared the stored row to the CONSTANT that produced it (widen the constant, both sides move); (3) `"_fits_fantasy_budget" in src` was satisfied by the function's own DEFINITION (stayed green with every caller deleted); (4) a disclaimer-forbidding substring scan was NEGATION-BLIND and fired on our OWN honest hedge ('it is not a projected finish') → the cheapest way to pass it was to DELETE the hedge that makes the surface honest; make such scans negation-aware. ⭐ And the product rule: a bare RANK is what a reader converts into a projected finish — the rank must name its own measure in the same breath ('Kth on projected starting points today'), and caveats must render UNCONDITIONALLY above the table (a caveat behind a click didn't render).
- **🧩 THREE NF-FRESH2 REUSABLE FINDINGS (2026-08-15):** (a) **THREE entitlement allowlists silently STRIP a new served field** (`_PUBLIC_PROJECTIONS_META_FIELDS`, `_PUBLIC_MANIFEST_FIELDS`, `_PUBLIC_MANIFEST_PROJECTION_FIELDS`) — a new served field must join ALL THREE in the same change or it's dropped on serialize with no error (the E9.41 dropped-field class, on the allowlist side). (b) **`croniter` is NOT installed on the box** (nor locally — Dagster 1.13 vendors its own) → a schedule/cron check must use `dagster._utils.schedules.cron_string_iterator` (the engine that actually fires), never `croniter`. (c) **a single build-date rendered over inputs of MULTIPLE vintages HIDES staleness** — NF-FRESH2's first publish stamped `depth_chart_as_of` 19 days stale (the laptop marts hadn't rebuilt since 07-27) and it was STRUCTURALLY INVISIBLE under one `built <date>`; per-input as-of stamps made it visible on the first run ('staleness must be visible, not inferable' is a shippable guard, not a slogan). ⭐ And a deploy-verification reinforcement (E9.60/E8.6 family): a follow-up commit pushed to an ALREADY-MERGED branch does NOT join the merged PR, and `gh pr checks <n>` happily serves the merged SHA's green checks → compare `headRefOid` against `git rev-parse HEAD` before trusting any green.
- **🐳 THREE CI/PERF LESSONS FROM THE E2E CONTAINER FIX (E9.66, 2026-08-15):** (a) ⭐ **A `run:` STEP INSIDE A `container:` DEFAULTS TO `sh` (dash), NOT bash** — so `set -o pipefail` and other bashisms die, and the step can FAIL CLOSED but NAME THE WRONG CAUSE (E9.66's pin-guard died at `pipefail` and reported a browser mismatch it never actually checked). Declare `shell: bash` once in the job's `defaults.run`. (b) ⭐ **MEASURE THE DISTRIBUTION BEFORE SIZING A PERF/CI FIX — a single bad observation is not the mean.** The card's premise ('`install-deps` takes 10m+, containerizing reclaims ~10 min/run') was the **92nd percentile** of a step whose median was **13s** over 49 runs (max 640s) — the hazard was VARIANCE, not mean, and '~10 min/run' overstated the saving ~45×; the right fix targets the TAIL (determinism), not the mean (sibling of the ESPN-PRUNER 're-measure a load-bearing number' + E11.24 'measure before crediting' family). And the load-bearing find: `playwright install-deps` was NEVER necessary — `ubuntu-latest` already ships Chrome, so chromium's libs were present and the step that occasionally hung 10 min did no work on 45 of 49 runs. (c) **CPU-BOUND ON A 4-vCPU RUNNER → MORE RUNNERS (sharding), NOT MORE WORKERS** — 2→4 Playwright workers bought only 8% wall-clock while summed per-test time went +40% (contention); shard across runners, because a worker bump on a saturated runner just adds contention. ⚠️ And when a fix DEVIATES from the operator's stated approach on MEASUREMENT (E9.66 DECLINED the requested branch→dev smoke-tiering because sharding made the full suite ≈ a smoke tier's cost, so tiering would only defer regressions to the promotion PR), RECORD it as a declined-with-reason decision the operator can reverse, never a silent omission.
- **🧪 THREE FRONTEND CI/E2E GOTCHAS FROM NF-C6P2 (2026-08-15):** (a) **`locator.count()` does NOT auto-retry** — it reads the DOM once, so a not-yet-rendered element passes LOCALLY and fails under parallel workers sharing a runner (a CI-only flake); use visibility-first assertions + `expect.poll`, verified under `--workers=2 --repeat-each=2`. (b) **a Radix Picker silently no-ops Playwright's `selectOption`** (it is not a native `<select>`) → drive it by trigger-click + option-click in the E2E; and a raw `<select>` trips the mobile-form-control guard → use the Radix Picker. (c) ⭐ **a `CONFLICTING` PR produces ZERO GitHub Actions runs** — when the merge commit can't be computed GitHub creates no `pull_request` run at all, so the PR shows no RED checks and reads deceptively clean; "no failing checks" ≠ green (the E9.60 "a run fires on the push, not the PR" family). Rebase/resolve to restore the runs. (This bit NF-C6P2 after E5.9 merged a changelog item into the SAME week block — the changelog-guard auto-merge conflict, the recurring same-week-block collision.)
- **🧪 THREE VERIFICATION-HYGIENE TRAPS FROM ONE FRONTEND SHIP (E9.60, 2026-08-09):** (a) ⭐ **ADDING A NEW STORY'S REQUIREMENT TO AN OLDER STORY'S GUARD CLAUSE COUPLES THEM** — an MLB-door requirement was added into an E9.58 clause; when the MLB decision later reversed, an E9.58 test failed for a reason unrelated to anything E9.58 defends. RE-ANCHORING an existing property onto a new implementation is fine; ADDING a new requirement to an old clause is not — a guard should fail only for the story it defends. (b) **`gh pr checks` CAN SERVE THE PREVIOUS COMMIT'S RUN** — reported 14/14 green while the HEAD commit's run had actually FAILED; confirm the run SHA matches HEAD before trusting a green. (c) **A WORKFLOW RUN FIRES ON THE PUSH, NOT THE PR** — a follow-up commit orphaned on an already-merged branch showed green CI while belonging to no open PR (the E8.6 already-merged-branch class); green CI is NOT evidence a PR exists. Also (frontend/CSS): a mobile viewport height cap MUST be `dvh`, not `vh` — an iOS-Safari `vh` cap is ~60-100px too tall and the last menu item stays unreachable (the bug surviving its own fix).
